using dgtl::mir::ABICallLayout;
using dgtl::mir::ABIConvention;
using dgtl::mir::ABIValueClass;

enum class BackendDriverTarget : unsigned char {
    arm64,
    x64,
    nasm,
    wasm,
    elfobj,
    staticlib,
    coffobj,
    cofflib,
    macho,
    arm32,
    riscv64,
    cuda,
    rocm,
};

struct BackendDriverRequest {
    std::string dgtl_executable_path;
    BackendDriverTarget target = BackendDriverTarget::arm64;
    std::string output_path;
    bool quiet = true;
    bool no_print = true;
    std::string ir_mode = "int";
};

struct BackendDriverResult {
    bool ok = false;
    int exit_code = -1;
    std::string backend_name;
    std::string output_path;
    std::string command_line;
    std::string message;
};

struct BackendDriverABIBridgeConfig {
    // Client may provide a callback to install external hooks (optional)
    void (*install_external_intrinsic_hooks)() = nullptr;
    // Client may provide a callback to register adapter signatures (optional)
    void (*register_adapter_signatures)(dgtl::core::DGTLABIRegistry&) = nullptr;
    dgtl::core::DGTLCallValidationOptions call_validation =
        dgtl::core::DGTLCallValidationOptions::lenient_runtime_intrinsics();
    ABIConvention convention = ABIConvention::SystemV_x64;
};

[[nodiscard]] inline ABIConvention backend_target_abi_convention(BackendDriverTarget target) noexcept {
    switch (target) {
    case BackendDriverTarget::arm64:
    case BackendDriverTarget::macho:
        return ABIConvention::ARM64_EABI;
    case BackendDriverTarget::wasm:
        return ABIConvention::WebAssembly;
    case BackendDriverTarget::coffobj:
    case BackendDriverTarget::cofflib:
        return ABIConvention::Microsoft_x64;
    case BackendDriverTarget::x64:
    case BackendDriverTarget::nasm:
#ifdef _WIN32
        return ABIConvention::Microsoft_x64;
#else
        return ABIConvention::SystemV_x64;
#endif
    case BackendDriverTarget::arm32:
        return ABIConvention::ARM32_AAPCS;
    case BackendDriverTarget::riscv64:
        return ABIConvention::RISCV64_LP64D;
    case BackendDriverTarget::cuda:
        return ABIConvention::CUDA_PTX;
    case BackendDriverTarget::rocm:
        return ABIConvention::ROCM_AMDGCN;
    case BackendDriverTarget::elfobj:
    case BackendDriverTarget::staticlib:
    default:
        return ABIConvention::SystemV_x64;
    }
}

inline void install_default_external_intrinsic_hooks() noexcept {
    // Intentionally empty in DGTL core. Client adapters register hooks via
    // BackendDriverABIBridgeConfig callbacks.
}

inline void seed_default_runtime_abi_registry(
    dgtl::core::DGTLABIRegistry& abi,
    const BackendDriverABIBridgeConfig& cfg = BackendDriverABIBridgeConfig{}) {
    if (cfg.install_external_intrinsic_hooks) {
        cfg.install_external_intrinsic_hooks();
    }
    if (cfg.register_adapter_signatures) {
        cfg.register_adapter_signatures(abi);
    }
}

[[nodiscard]] inline ABIValueClass to_lowering_abi_value_class(
    dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass value_class) noexcept {
    switch (value_class) {
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::f32:
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::f64:
        return ABIValueClass::FloatingPoint;
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::aggregate:
        return ABIValueClass::Memory;
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::none:
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::i32:
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::i64:
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::pointer:
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::custom:
    default:
        return ABIValueClass::IntegerOrPointer;
    }
}

[[nodiscard]] inline uint32_t dgtl_abi_value_size_bytes(
    dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass value_class) noexcept {
    switch (value_class) {
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::i32:
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::f32:
        return 4u;
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::none:
        return 0u;
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::aggregate:
        return 16u;
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::i64:
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::f64:
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::pointer:
    case dgtl::core::DGTLABIValueClass::custom:
    default:
        return 8u;
    }
}

[[nodiscard]] inline ABICallLayout lower_call_signature_layout(
    const dgtl::core::DGTLCallSignature& signature,
    ABIConvention convention,
    const BackendDriverABIBridgeConfig& cfg = BackendDriverABIBridgeConfig{}) {
    dgtl::mir::ABI_Lowering lowering(convention);

    std::vector<ABIValueClass> classes;
    classes.reserve(signature.parameters.size());

    std::vector<uint32_t> sizes;
    sizes.reserve(signature.parameters.size());

    for (const auto& param : signature.parameters) {
        classes.push_back(to_lowering_abi_value_class(param.value_class));
        sizes.push_back(dgtl_abi_value_size_bytes(param.value_class));
    }

    const size_t fixed_param_count = signature.parameters.size();

    (void)cfg.call_validation;
    return lowering.plan_call_layout(
        classes,
        sizes,
        to_lowering_abi_value_class(signature.return_value.value_class),
        signature.is_variadic,
        fixed_param_count);
}

inline std::vector<BackendDriverTarget> all_backend_driver_targets() {
    return {
        BackendDriverTarget::arm64,
        BackendDriverTarget::x64,
        BackendDriverTarget::nasm,
        BackendDriverTarget::wasm,
        BackendDriverTarget::elfobj,
        BackendDriverTarget::staticlib,
        BackendDriverTarget::coffobj,
        BackendDriverTarget::cofflib,
        BackendDriverTarget::macho,
        BackendDriverTarget::arm32,
        BackendDriverTarget::riscv64,
        BackendDriverTarget::cuda,
        BackendDriverTarget::rocm,
    };
}

struct IRStreamEmitRequest {
    std::string module_name;
    std::string output_dir = "build/out";
    const dgtl::ir::IRStream* stream = nullptr;
    const dgtl::semantic::SymbolTable* symbols = nullptr;
    const std::unordered_map<uint32_t, std::string>* slot_to_callee = nullptr;
    bool emit_all = false;
    std::optional<BackendDriverTarget> target;
};

struct FrontendEmitResult {
    bool ok = false;
    std::vector<BackendDriverResult> per_target;
    std::string message;
};

namespace detail {

inline std::vector<std::pair<std::string, dgtl::ir::IRStream>> split_stream_by_exported_labels(
    const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream,
    const std::unordered_map<uint32_t, std::string>& slot_to_callee) {
    std::vector<std::pair<std::string, dgtl::ir::IRStream>> module_streams;
    if (stream.is_null() || stream.size() == 0 || slot_to_callee.empty()) {
        return module_streams;
    }

    std::size_t function_count = 0;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < stream.size(); ++i) {
        const auto& node = stream.get_node(i);
        if (node.opcode == dgtl::ir::OpCode::LABEL && slot_to_callee.contains(node.result_id)) {
            ++function_count;
        }
    }
    if (function_count == 0) {
        return module_streams;
    }

    module_streams.reserve(function_count);
    std::size_t current_index = static_cast<std::size_t>(-1);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < stream.size(); ++i) {
        const auto& node = stream.get_node(i);
        if (node.opcode == dgtl::ir::OpCode::LABEL) {
            const auto fn_it = slot_to_callee.find(node.result_id);
            if (fn_it != slot_to_callee.end()) {
                module_streams.emplace_back(
                    fn_it->second,
                    dgtl::ir::IRStream::allocate_transactional(std::max<std::size_t>(stream.size(), 64)));
                current_index = module_streams.size() - 1;
            }
        }

        if (current_index != static_cast<std::size_t>(-1)) {
            module_streams[current_index].second.push_back(node);
        }
    }

    return module_streams;
}

inline bool emit_arm64_direct(const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream,
                              const dgtl::semantic::SymbolTable& symbols,
                              std::string& out_text) {
    dgtl::isa::ARM64Emitter emitter(stream, symbols);
    dgtl::isa::ARM64Emitter::Config cfg;
    cfg.verbose_asm = true;
    std::ostringstream oss;
    emitter.emit_assembly(oss, cfg);
    out_text = oss.str();
    return !out_text.empty();
}

inline bool emit_x64_direct(const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream,
                            std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes,
                            std::string& out_text) {
    dgtl::isa::X64StrikeEmitter emitter;
    out_bytes.clear();
    if (!emitter.process_stream(stream, out_bytes) || out_bytes.empty()) {
        return false;
    }
    out_text = dgtl::backend::driver::shared::bytes_to_hex(out_bytes);
    return true;
}

inline bool emit_x64_direct(const dgtl::machine::MachineFunction& function,
                            std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes,
                            std::string& out_text) {
    dgtl::isa::X64StrikeEmitter emitter;
    out_bytes.clear();
    dgtl::machine::MachineFunction function_copy = function;
    if (!emitter.process_function(function_copy, out_bytes) || out_bytes.empty()) {
        return false;
    }
    out_text = dgtl::backend::driver::shared::bytes_to_hex(out_bytes);
    return true;
}

inline bool emit_nasm_direct(const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream,
                             const dgtl::semantic::SymbolTable& symbols,
                             std::string& out_text) {
    dgtl::isa::DGTLNasmEmitter emitter;
    emitter.emit(stream, symbols);
    out_text = emitter.get_output();
    return !out_text.empty();
}

inline bool emit_nasm_direct(const dgtl::machine::MachineFunction& function,
                             const dgtl::semantic::SymbolTable* symbols,
                             std::string& out_text) {
    dgtl::isa::DGTLNasmEmitter emitter;
    emitter.emit(function, symbols);
    out_text = emitter.get_output();
    return !out_text.empty();
}

inline bool emit_wasm_direct(const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream,
                             std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes,
                             std::string& out_text) {
    dgtl::core::TargetMachine target(dgtl::core::ArchType::WASM_SIMD);
    dgtl::mir::isel::InstructionSelectorProgram selector;
    dgtl::machine::MachineFunction function;
    std::string diagnostic;
    if (!dgtl::isa::wasm::WasmMIRBoundary::build(stream, target, selector, function, diagnostic)) {
        return false;
    }
    return emit_wasm_direct(function, out_bytes, out_text);
}

inline bool emit_wasm_direct(const dgtl::machine::MachineFunction& function,
                             std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes,
                             std::string& out_text) {
    dgtl::exec::DGTLWasmEncoder encoder;
    encoder.encode(function);
    out_bytes = encoder.get_binary();
    if (out_bytes.empty()) return false;
    out_text = dgtl::backend::driver::shared::bytes_to_hex(out_bytes);
    return true;
}

inline bool emit_elfobj_direct(const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream, std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes) {
    dgtl::exec::DGTLLinker linker;
    return linker.build_elf_relocatable_binary(stream, out_bytes, "DGTL_kernel_entry");
}

inline bool emit_staticlib_direct(const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream, std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes) {
    dgtl::exec::DGTLLinker linker;
    return linker.build_static_archive_binary(stream, out_bytes, "module.o");
}

inline bool emit_coffobj_direct(const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream, std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes) {
    dgtl::exec::DGTLLinker linker;
    return linker.build_coff_relocatable_binary(stream, out_bytes, "DGTL_kernel_entry");
}

inline bool emit_cofflib_direct(const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream, std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes) {
    dgtl::exec::DGTLLinker linker;
    return linker.build_coff_archive_binary(stream, out_bytes, "module.obj", "DGTL_kernel_entry");
}

inline bool emit_macho_direct(const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream,
                              const dgtl::semantic::SymbolTable& symbols,
                              std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes) {
    dgtl::isa::MachOEmitter emitter(stream, symbols);
    auto result = emitter.emit_to_binary();
    if (!result.success) return false;
    out_bytes = std::move(result.binary_data);
    return !out_bytes.empty();
}

} // namespace detail

inline const char* backend_driver_target_name(BackendDriverTarget target) {
    switch (target) {
    case BackendDriverTarget::arm64: return "arm64";
    case BackendDriverTarget::x64: return "x64";
    case BackendDriverTarget::nasm: return "nasm";
    case BackendDriverTarget::wasm: return "wasm";
    case BackendDriverTarget::elfobj: return "elfobj";
    case BackendDriverTarget::staticlib: return "staticlib";
    case BackendDriverTarget::coffobj: return "coffobj";
    case BackendDriverTarget::cofflib: return "cofflib";
    case BackendDriverTarget::macho: return "macho";
    case BackendDriverTarget::arm32: return "arm32";
    case BackendDriverTarget::riscv64: return "riscv64";
    case BackendDriverTarget::cuda: return "cuda";
    case BackendDriverTarget::rocm: return "rocm";
    default: return "arm64";
    }
}

inline const char* backend_driver_target_extension(BackendDriverTarget target) {
    switch (target) {
    case BackendDriverTarget::arm64: return ".s";
    case BackendDriverTarget::x64: return ".bin";
    case BackendDriverTarget::nasm: return ".asm";
    case BackendDriverTarget::wasm: return ".wasm";
    case BackendDriverTarget::elfobj: return ".o";
    case BackendDriverTarget::staticlib: return ".a";
    case BackendDriverTarget::coffobj: return ".obj";
    case BackendDriverTarget::cofflib: return ".lib";
    case BackendDriverTarget::macho: return ".macho";
    case BackendDriverTarget::arm32: return ".s";
    case BackendDriverTarget::riscv64: return ".s";
    case BackendDriverTarget::cuda: return ".cu";
    case BackendDriverTarget::rocm: return ".hip";
    default: return ".out";
    }
}

inline bool parse_backend_driver_target(std::string value, BackendDriverTarget& out) {
    value = dgtl::backend::driver::shared::to_lower_ascii(
        dgtl::backend::driver::shared::trim_ascii(std::move(value)));

    if (value == "arm64") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::arm64;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "x64") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::x64;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "nasm") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::nasm;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "wasm") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::wasm;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "elfobj" || value == "obj" || value == "o") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::elfobj;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "staticlib" || value == "archive" || value == "a" || value == "lib") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::staticlib;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "coffobj" || value == "coff") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::coffobj;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "cofflib" || value == "coffarchive") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::cofflib;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "macho" || value == "mach-o" || value == "macho64") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::macho;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "arm32") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::arm32;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "riscv64" || value == "riscv" || value == "rv64") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::riscv64;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "cuda" || value == "ptx" || value == "nvptx") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::cuda;
        return true;
    }
    if (value == "rocm" || value == "amdgcn" || value == "hip") {
        out = BackendDriverTarget::rocm;
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

[[nodiscard]] inline bool backend_driver_target_is_supported(BackendDriverTarget target) noexcept {
    switch (target) {
    case BackendDriverTarget::arm64:
    case BackendDriverTarget::x64:
    case BackendDriverTarget::nasm:
    case BackendDriverTarget::wasm:
    case BackendDriverTarget::elfobj:
    case BackendDriverTarget::staticlib:
    case BackendDriverTarget::coffobj:
    case BackendDriverTarget::cofflib:
    case BackendDriverTarget::macho:
    case BackendDriverTarget::arm32:
    case BackendDriverTarget::riscv64:
    case BackendDriverTarget::cuda:
    case BackendDriverTarget::rocm:
        return true;
    default:
        return false;
    }
}

[[nodiscard]] inline bool backend_driver_target_emits_binary_artifact(BackendDriverTarget target) noexcept {
    switch (target) {
    case BackendDriverTarget::x64:
    case BackendDriverTarget::wasm:
    case BackendDriverTarget::elfobj:
    case BackendDriverTarget::staticlib:
    case BackendDriverTarget::coffobj:
    case BackendDriverTarget::cofflib:
    case BackendDriverTarget::macho:
        return true;
    case BackendDriverTarget::arm64:
    case BackendDriverTarget::nasm:
    case BackendDriverTarget::arm32:
    case BackendDriverTarget::riscv64:
    case BackendDriverTarget::cuda:
    case BackendDriverTarget::rocm:
    default:
        return false;
    }
}

inline std::string quote_cmd_arg(std::string_view value) {
    std::string out;
    out.reserve(value.size() + 2);
    out.push_back('"');
    for (char c : value) {
        if (c == '"') {
            out += "\\\"";
        } else {
            out.push_back(c);
        }
    }
    out.push_back('"');
    return out;
}

inline BackendDriverResult run_backend_driver(const BackendDriverRequest& req) {
    BackendDriverResult result;
    result.backend_name = backend_driver_target_name(req.target);
    result.output_path = req.output_path;

    if (req.dgtl_executable_path.empty()) {
        result.message = "DGTL executable path is empty";
        return result;
    }

    const std::filesystem::path exe_path = std::filesystem::absolute(std::filesystem::path(req.dgtl_executable_path)).lexically_normal();
    if (!std::filesystem::exists(exe_path)) {
        result.message = "DGTL executable not found at " + exe_path.string();
        return result;
    }

    if (req.output_path.empty()) {
        result.message = "Output path is empty";
        return result;
    }

    const std::filesystem::path out_path = std::filesystem::absolute(std::filesystem::path(req.output_path)).lexically_normal();
    if (out_path.has_parent_path()) {
        std::error_code ec;
        std::filesystem::create_directories(out_path.parent_path(), ec);
        if (ec) {
            result.message = "Failed to create output directory for " + req.output_path;
            return result;
        }
    }

    std::ostringstream cmd;
#ifdef _WIN32
    auto quote_ps_single = [](std::string_view value) {
        std::string out;
        out.reserve(value.size() + 2);
        for (char c : value) {
            if (c == '\'') {
                out += "''";
            } else {
                out.push_back(c);
            }
        }
        return out;
    };

    cmd << "powershell -NoProfile -NonInteractive -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command \"& '"
        << quote_ps_single(exe_path.string())
        << "' --backend " << backend_driver_target_name(req.target)
        << " --ir-mode " << req.ir_mode
        << " --emit-output '" << quote_ps_single(out_path.string()) << "'";

    if (req.no_print) {
        cmd << " --no-print";
    }
    if (req.quiet) {
        cmd << " --quiet";
    }
    cmd << "\"";
#else
    cmd << quote_cmd_arg(exe_path.string())
        << " --backend " << backend_driver_target_name(req.target)
        << " --ir-mode " << req.ir_mode
        << " --emit-output " << quote_cmd_arg(out_path.string());

    if (req.no_print) {
        cmd << " --no-print";
    }
    if (req.quiet) {
        cmd << " --quiet";
    }
#endif

    result.command_line = cmd.str();
    result.exit_code = std::system(result.command_line.c_str());
    result.ok = (result.exit_code == 0);

    if (!result.ok && result.message.empty()) {
        result.message = "DGTL backend driver failed with exit code " + std::to_string(result.exit_code);
    }

    return result;
}

inline bool validate_backend_driver_request(const BackendDriverRequest& req,
                                            std::string* error = nullptr) {
    if (req.dgtl_executable_path.empty()) {
        if (error) {
            *error = "DGTL executable path is empty";
        }
        return false;
    }

    const std::filesystem::path exe_path =
        std::filesystem::absolute(std::filesystem::path(req.dgtl_executable_path)).lexically_normal();
    if (!std::filesystem::exists(exe_path)) {
        if (error) {
            *error = "DGTL executable not found at " + exe_path.string();
        }
        return false;
    }

    if (req.output_path.empty()) {
        if (error) {
            *error = "Output path is empty";
        }
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

inline BackendDriverRequest make_backend_driver_request(std::string_view dgtl_executable_path,
                                                        BackendDriverTarget target,
                                                        std::string_view output_path,
                                                        bool quiet = true,
                                                        bool no_print = true,
                                                        std::string_view ir_mode = "int") {
    BackendDriverRequest req;
    req.dgtl_executable_path = std::string(dgtl_executable_path);
    req.target = target;
    req.output_path = std::string(output_path);
    req.quiet = quiet;
    req.no_print = no_print;
    req.ir_mode = std::string(ir_mode);
    return req;
}

struct MachineFunctionEmitRequest {
    std::string module_name;
    std::string output_dir = "build/out";
    const machine::MachineFunction* function = nullptr;
    const std::vector<const machine::MachineFunction*>* module_functions = nullptr;
    const dgtl::object::ObjectFile* object_file = nullptr;
    bool emit_all = false;
    std::optional<BackendDriverTarget> target;
};

inline FrontendEmitResult emit_machine_function(const MachineFunctionEmitRequest& req) {
    FrontendEmitResult result;

    if (req.function == nullptr && req.module_functions == nullptr && req.object_file == nullptr) {
        result.message = "MachineFunction request has no function or object file";
        return result;
    }
    if (req.module_name.empty()) {
        result.message = "module name is empty";
        return result;
    }

    std::vector<BackendDriverTarget> targets;
    if (req.emit_all) {
        targets = all_backend_driver_targets();
    } else if (req.target.has_value()) {
        targets.push_back(*req.target);
    } else {
        result.message = "Neither emit_all nor a specific target was provided";
        return result;
    }

    bool all_ok = true;
    for (const auto target : targets) {
        BackendDriverResult per{};
        per.backend_name = backend_driver_target_name(target);
        const std::string filename = req.module_name +
            ((target == BackendDriverTarget::wasm || target == BackendDriverTarget::elfobj ||
              target == BackendDriverTarget::staticlib || target == BackendDriverTarget::coffobj ||
              target == BackendDriverTarget::cofflib || target == BackendDriverTarget::macho)
                 ? std::string(backend_driver_target_extension(target))
                 : std::string(".") + backend_driver_target_name(target) + backend_driver_target_extension(target));
        const std::filesystem::path out_path = std::filesystem::path(req.output_dir) / filename;
        std::error_code ec;
        std::filesystem::create_directories(out_path.parent_path(), ec);

        std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
        std::string text;
        bool ok = false;

        switch (target) {
            case BackendDriverTarget::x64: {
                if (req.function) {
                    ok = detail::emit_x64_direct(*req.function, bytes, text) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                } else if (req.object_file) {
                    dgtl::object::writers::PEObjectWriter writer;
                    ok = writer.write(*req.object_file, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::arm64: {
                if (req.function) {
                    text = "// DGTL ARM64 MIR Emission: " + req.function->name() + "\n";
                    for (const auto& blk : req.function->blocks()) {
                        text += blk.name() + ":\n";
                        for (const auto& mi : blk.instructions()) {
                            text += "  " + mi.to_string() + "\n";
                        }
                    }
                    ok = shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::arm32: {
                if (req.function) {
                    text = "// DGTL ARM32 MIR Emission: " + req.function->name() + "\n";
                    for (const auto& blk : req.function->blocks()) {
                        text += blk.name() + ":\n";
                        for (const auto& mi : blk.instructions()) {
                            text += "  " + mi.to_string() + "\n";
                        }
                    }
                    ok = shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::riscv64: {
                if (req.function) {
                    text = "// DGTL RISCV64 MIR Emission: " + req.function->name() + "\n";
                    for (const auto& blk : req.function->blocks()) {
                        text += blk.name() + ":\n";
                        for (const auto& mi : blk.instructions()) {
                            text += "  " + mi.to_string() + "\n";
                        }
                    }
                    ok = shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::wasm: {
                dgtl::object::writers::WASMBinaryWriter writer;
                if (req.function) {
                    ok = writer.write_machine_function(*req.function, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                } else if (req.object_file) {
                    ok = writer.write(*req.object_file, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::elfobj: {
                dgtl::object::writers::ELFObjectWriter writer;
                if (req.function) {
                    ok = writer.write_machine_function(*req.function, bytes, req.function->name()) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                } else if (req.object_file) {
                    ok = writer.write(*req.object_file, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::staticlib: {
                dgtl::object::writers::ELFObjectWriter writer;
                std::vector<uint8_t> obj_bytes;
                if (req.function) {
                    ok = writer.write_machine_function(*req.function, obj_bytes, req.function->name()) &&
                         writer.build_archive(obj_bytes, bytes, "module.o") &&
                         shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                } else if (req.object_file) {
                    ok = writer.write(*req.object_file, obj_bytes) &&
                         writer.build_archive(obj_bytes, bytes, "module.o") &&
                         shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::coffobj: {
                dgtl::object::writers::PEObjectWriter writer;
                if (req.function) {
                    ok = writer.write_machine_function(*req.function, bytes, req.function->name()) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                } else if (req.object_file) {
                    ok = writer.write(*req.object_file, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::cofflib: {
                dgtl::object::writers::PEObjectWriter writer;
                std::vector<uint8_t> obj_bytes;
                if (req.function) {
                    ok = writer.write_machine_function(*req.function, obj_bytes, req.function->name()) &&
                         writer.build_archive(obj_bytes, bytes, "module.obj") &&
                         shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                } else if (req.object_file) {
                    ok = writer.write(*req.object_file, obj_bytes) &&
                         writer.build_archive(obj_bytes, bytes, "module.obj") &&
                         shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::macho: {
                dgtl::object::writers::MachOObjectWriter writer;
                if (req.function) {
                    ok = writer.write_machine_function(*req.function, bytes, req.function->name()) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                } else if (req.object_file) {
                    ok = writer.write(*req.object_file, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::cuda: {
                if (req.function) {
                    text = ".version 8.0\n.target sm_90\n.address_size 64\n.visible .entry " + req.function->name() + "() {\n";
                    for (const auto& blk : req.function->blocks()) {
                        text += blk.name() + ":\n";
                        for (const auto& mi : blk.instructions()) {
                            text += "  " + mi.to_string() + ";\n";
                        }
                    }
                    text += "}\n";
                    ok = shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::rocm: {
                if (req.function) {
                    text = ".text\n.globl " + req.function->name() + "\n.p2align 8\n" + req.function->name() + ":\n";
                    for (const auto& blk : req.function->blocks()) {
                        text += blk.name() + ":\n";
                        for (const auto& mi : blk.instructions()) {
                            text += "  " + mi.to_string() + "\n";
                        }
                    }
                    text += "  s_endpgm\n";
                    ok = shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::nasm: {
                if (req.function) {
                    text = "global " + req.function->name() + "\nsection .text\n" + req.function->name() + ":\n";
                    for (const auto& blk : req.function->blocks()) {
                        text += blk.name() + ":\n";
                        for (const auto& mi : blk.instructions()) {
                            text += "  " + mi.to_string() + "\n";
                        }
                    }
                    ok = shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text);
                }
                break;
            }
            default:
                ok = false;
                break;
        }

        per.ok = ok;
        per.output_path = out_path.string();
        per.exit_code = ok ? 0 : -1;
        if (!ok) {
            per.message = "MachineFunction backend emission failed";
            all_ok = false;
        }
        result.per_target.push_back(std::move(per));
    }

    result.ok = all_ok;
    if (!result.ok && result.message.empty()) {
        result.message = "one or more backend driver emissions failed";
    }
    return result;
}

inline FrontendEmitResult emit_ir_stream(const IRStreamEmitRequest& req) {
    FrontendEmitResult result;

    if (req.stream == nullptr || req.stream->is_null()) {
        result.message = "IR stream is null";
        return result;
    }
    if (req.module_name.empty()) {
        result.message = "module name is empty";
        return result;
    }

    dgtl::core::DGTL_Arena local_arena(1, 64);
    dgtl::semantic::SymbolTable local_symbols(local_arena);
    const auto& symbols = req.symbols != nullptr ? *req.symbols : local_symbols;

    std::vector<BackendDriverTarget> targets;
    if (req.emit_all) {
        targets = all_backend_driver_targets();
    } else if (req.target.has_value()) {
        targets.push_back(*req.target);
    } else {
        result.message = "Neither emit_all nor a specific target was provided";
        return result;
    }

    bool all_ok = true;
    for (const auto target : targets) {
        BackendDriverResult per{};
        per.backend_name = backend_driver_target_name(target);
        const std::string filename = req.module_name +
            ((target == BackendDriverTarget::wasm || target == BackendDriverTarget::elfobj ||
              target == BackendDriverTarget::staticlib || target == BackendDriverTarget::coffobj ||
              target == BackendDriverTarget::cofflib || target == BackendDriverTarget::macho)
                 ? std::string(backend_driver_target_extension(target))
                 : std::string(".") + backend_driver_target_name(target) + backend_driver_target_extension(target));
        const std::filesystem::path out_path = std::filesystem::path(req.output_dir) / filename;
        std::error_code ec;
        std::filesystem::create_directories(out_path.parent_path(), ec);

        std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
        std::string text;
        bool ok = false;
        switch (target) {
            case BackendDriverTarget::arm64: ok = detail::emit_arm64_direct(*req.stream, symbols, text) && shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::x64: ok = detail::emit_x64_direct(*req.stream, bytes, text) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::nasm: ok = detail::emit_nasm_direct(*req.stream, symbols, text) && shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::wasm: ok = detail::emit_wasm_direct(*req.stream, bytes, text) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::elfobj: ok = detail::emit_elfobj_direct(*req.stream, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::staticlib: ok = detail::emit_staticlib_direct(*req.stream, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::coffobj: {
                if (req.slot_to_callee != nullptr && !req.slot_to_callee->empty()) {
                    dgtl::exec::DGTLLinker linker;
                    auto module_streams = detail::split_stream_by_exported_labels(*req.stream, *req.slot_to_callee);
                    if (module_streams.size() > 1) {
                        ok = linker.build_module_coff_with_relocs(module_streams, bytes, *req.slot_to_callee) &&
                             shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                    } else {
                        const auto relocs = linker.collect_call_relocs(*req.stream, *req.slot_to_callee);
                        ok = linker.build_coff_relocatable_binary_with_relocs(*req.stream, bytes, "DGTL_kernel_entry", relocs) &&
                             shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                    }
                    if (!linker.fallback_diagnostics().empty()) {
                        std::fprintf(stderr,
                                     "[DGTL][linker] %zu fallback opcode(s) while emitting %s\n",
                                     linker.fallback_diagnostics().size(),
                                     out_path.string().c_str());
                        for (const auto& diag : linker.fallback_diagnostics()) {
                            std::fprintf(stderr, "  - %s\n", diag.c_str());
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    ok = detail::emit_coffobj_direct(*req.stream, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes);
                }
                break;
            }
            case BackendDriverTarget::cofflib: ok = detail::emit_cofflib_direct(*req.stream, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::macho: ok = detail::emit_macho_direct(*req.stream, symbols, bytes) && shared::write_binary_file(out_path.string(), bytes); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::arm32: ok = shared::emit_arm32_text(text) && shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::riscv64: ok = shared::emit_riscv64_text(text) && shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::cuda: ok = shared::emit_cuda_text(*req.stream, text, nullptr) && shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text); break;
            case BackendDriverTarget::rocm: ok = shared::emit_rocm_text(*req.stream, text, nullptr) && shared::write_text_file(out_path.string(), text); break;
            default: ok = false; break;
        }

        per.ok = ok;
        per.output_path = out_path.string();
        per.exit_code = ok ? 0 : -1;
        if (!ok) {
            per.message = "IRStream backend emission failed";
            all_ok = false;
        }
        result.per_target.push_back(std::move(per));
    }

    result.ok = all_ok;
    if (!result.ok && result.message.empty()) {
        result.message = "one or more backend driver emissions failed";
    }
    return result;
}

} // namespace dgtl::backend::driver

                                            BackendDriverTarget target,

    }

        using MachineFunctionEmitRequest = dgtl::backend::driver::MachineFunctionEmitRequest;

    inline auto emit_machine_function(const MachineFunctionEmitRequest& req) {

        return dgtl::backend::driver::emit_ir_stream(req);
    }
}

#include <cstdlib>

#include <cctype>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>

#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <string>

#define DGTL_HAS_GPGPU_FUSION_ENGINE 0

export module dgtl.linker.utils;

import dgtl.ir.stream;
import dgtl.ir.analysis.polyhedral_scop_unified;
import dgtl.target;

export namespace dgtl::linker::driver::utils {

inline std::string to_lower_ascii(std::string value) {
    std::transform(value.begin(), value.end(), value.begin(), [](unsigned char ch) {
        return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(ch));
    });
    return value;
}

inline std::string trim_ascii(std::string value) {
    auto is_ws = [](unsigned char c) { return std::isspace(c) != 0; };
    while (!value.empty() && is_ws(static_cast<unsigned char>(value.back()))) {
        value.pop_back();
    }
    size_t begin = 0;
    while (begin < value.size() && is_ws(static_cast<unsigned char>(value[begin]))) {
        ++begin;
    }
    return value.substr(begin);
}

template <typename PolyhedralModeT>
inline bool parse_polyhedral_mode(std::string mode_str,
                                  PolyhedralModeT& out_mode,
                                  std::string& out_name) {
    mode_str = to_lower_ascii(std::move(mode_str));
    if (mode_str == "off") {
        out_mode = PolyhedralModeT::off;
        out_name = "off";
        return true;
    }
    if (mode_str == "safe") {
        out_mode = PolyhedralModeT::safe;
        out_name = "safe";
        return true;
    }
    if (mode_str == "aggressive") {
        out_mode = PolyhedralModeT::aggressive;
        out_name = "aggressive";
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

template <typename PolyhedralParallelPolicyT>
inline bool parse_polyhedral_parallel_policy(std::string policy_str,
                                             PolyhedralParallelPolicyT& out_policy,
                                             std::string& out_name) {
    policy_str = to_lower_ascii(std::move(policy_str));
    if (policy_str == "auto") {
        out_policy = PolyhedralParallelPolicyT::auto_policy;
        out_name = "auto";
        return true;
    }
    if (policy_str == "off") {
        out_policy = PolyhedralParallelPolicyT::off;
        out_name = "off";
        return true;
    }
    if (policy_str == "on") {
        out_policy = PolyhedralParallelPolicyT::on;
        out_name = "on";
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

inline std::string bytes_to_hex(const std::vector<uint8_t>& bytes) {
    static constexpr char kHex[] = "0123456789ABCDEF";
    std::string out;
    out.reserve(bytes.size() * 3);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < bytes.size(); ++i) {
        const uint8_t b = bytes[i];
        out.push_back(kHex[(b >> 4) & 0xF]);
        out.push_back(kHex[b & 0xF]);
        if (i + 1 < bytes.size()) {
            out.push_back(' ');
        }
    }
    return out;
}

inline bool write_text_file(const std::string& path, const std::string& content) {
    const std::filesystem::path p(path);
    if (p.has_parent_path()) {
        std::error_code ec;
        std::filesystem::create_directories(p.parent_path(), ec);
        if (ec) [[unlikely]] {
            return false;
        }
    }

    std::ofstream out(path, std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
    if (!out.is_open()) [[unlikely]] {
        return false;
    }

    out.write(content.data(), static_cast<std::streamsize>(content.size()));
    return out.good();
}

inline bool write_binary_file(const std::string& path, const std::vector<uint8_t>& bytes) {
    const std::filesystem::path p(path);
    if (p.has_parent_path()) {
        std::error_code ec;
        std::filesystem::create_directories(p.parent_path(), ec);
        if (ec) [[unlikely]] {
            return false;
        }
    }

    std::ofstream out(path, std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
    if (!out.is_open()) [[unlikely]] {
        return false;
    }

    if (!bytes.empty()) {
        out.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(bytes.data()), static_cast<std::streamsize>(bytes.size()));
    }

    return out.good();
}

inline bool emit_arm32_text(std::string& /*out_text*/) {
    return false; // ARM32 backend removed in modern DGTL pipeline
}

inline bool emit_riscv64_text(std::string& out_text) {
    std::vector<std::string> lines;
    lines.reserve(64);
    lines.push_back("# RISC-V 64 (RV64GC + V-extension) Emission");
    lines.push_back(".arch rv64gcv");
    lines.push_back("DGTL_riscv64_entry:");
    dgtl::isa::RiscV64GatherScatter::emit_gather(lines, 0, 0, 1, 4, true);
    dgtl::isa::RiscV64GatherScatter::emit_scatter(lines, 1, 0, 1, 4, true);
    lines.push_back("ret");

    std::ostringstream oss;
    for (const auto& line : lines) {
        oss << line << '\n';
    }
    out_text = oss.str();
    return !out_text.empty();
}

inline bool emit_cuda_text(
    const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream,
    std::string& out_text,
    const dgtl::ir::analysis::polyhedral::PolyhedralSchedule* schedule = nullptr) {
#if DGTL_HAS_GPGPU_FUSION_ENGINE
    dgtl::core::TargetMachine target(dgtl::core::ArchType::X86_64_AVX512);
    dgtl::dialect::GPGPUFusionEngine engine(dgtl::dialect::DeviceType::NVIDIA_PTX, target);
    if (schedule != nullptr && schedule->is_valid() && schedule->has_any_tiled_regions()) {
        out_text = engine.fuse_to_source_tiled(stream, *schedule);
    } else {
        out_text = engine.fuse_to_source(stream);
    }
    return !out_text.empty();
#else
    (void)schedule;
    std::ostringstream oss;
    oss << "// DGTL generated CUDA-style fallback from declarative op metadata\n";
    oss << "// GPU fusion engine header not present in source tree\n";
    oss << "extern \"C\" __global__ void dgtl_kernel() {\n";
    for (size_t i = 0; i < stream.size(); ++i) {
        const auto n = stream.get_node_readonly(i);
        if (auto* def = dgtl::ir::gen::find_generated_op_def(n.opcode)) {
            if ((def->interface_bits & static_cast<uint32_t>(dgtl::ir::gen::GenInterface::GPU)) != 0u) {
                oss << "  // gpu-op: " << def->name << "\n";
            }
        }
    }
    oss << "}\n";
    out_text = oss.str();
    return !out_text.empty();
#endif
}

inline bool emit_rocm_text(
    const dgtl::ir::IRStream& stream,
    std::string& out_text,
    const dgtl::ir::analysis::polyhedral::PolyhedralSchedule* schedule = nullptr) {
#if DGTL_HAS_GPGPU_FUSION_ENGINE
    dgtl::core::TargetMachine target(dgtl::core::ArchType::AARCH64_SVE);
    dgtl::dialect::GPGPUFusionEngine engine(dgtl::dialect::DeviceType::AMD_GCN_CDNA, target);
    if (schedule != nullptr && schedule->is_valid() && schedule->has_any_tiled_regions()) {
        out_text = engine.fuse_to_source_tiled(stream, *schedule);
    } else {
        out_text = engine.fuse_to_source(stream);
    }
    return !out_text.empty();
#else
    (void)schedule;
    std::ostringstream oss;
    oss << "// DGTL generated ROCm-style fallback from declarative op metadata\n";
    oss << "extern \"C\" __attribute__((global)) void dgtl_kernel() {\n";
    for (size_t i = 0; i < stream.size(); ++i) {
        const auto n = stream.get_node_readonly(i);
        if (auto* def = dgtl::ir::gen::find_generated_op_def(n.opcode)) {
            if ((def->interface_bits & static_cast<uint32_t>(dgtl::ir::gen::GenInterface::GPU)) != 0u) {
                oss << "  // gpu-op: " << def->name << "\n";
            }
        }
    }
    oss << "}\n";
    out_text = oss.str();
    return !out_text.empty();
#endif
}

} // namespace dgtl::backend::driver::shared

export module dgtl.linker.elf;

import dgtl.linker.backend;
import dgtl.object.writers.elf;
import dgtl.object.file;
import dgtl.mir;

export namespace dgtl::linker::elf {

class ELFLinker {
public:
    // Sovereign MIR-native linking
    bool link_relocatable(
        const machine::MachineFunction& fn,
        std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes,
        std::string_view entry_symbol = "DGTL_kernel_entry") const {
        dgtl::object::writers::ELFObjectWriter writer;
        return writer.write_machine_function(fn, out_bytes, entry_symbol);
    }

    bool link_relocatable(
        const dgtl::object::ObjectFile& obj,
        std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes) const {
        dgtl::object::writers::ELFObjectWriter writer;
        return writer.write(obj, out_bytes);
    }

    bool link_static_archive(
        const machine::MachineFunction& fn,
        std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes,
        std::string_view member_name = "module.o") const {
        std::vector<uint8_t> obj_bytes;
        dgtl::object::writers::ELFObjectWriter writer;
        if (!writer.write_machine_function(fn, obj_bytes, fn.name())) return false;
        return writer.build_archive(obj_bytes, out_bytes, member_name);
    }

    bool link_static_archive(
        const dgtl::object::ObjectFile& obj,
        std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes,
        std::string_view member_name = "module.o") const {
        std::vector<uint8_t> obj_bytes;
        dgtl::object::writers::ELFObjectWriter writer;
        if (!writer.write(obj, obj_bytes)) return false;
        return writer.build_archive(obj_bytes, out_bytes, member_name);
    }

    bool link_executable(
        const dgtl::object::ObjectFile& obj,
        std::vector<uint8_t>& out_bytes,
        uint64_t image_base = 0x400000) const {
        dgtl::object::writers::ELFObjectWriter writer;
        return writer.write_standalone_executable(obj, out_bytes, image_base);
    }

    // Backward-compatibility overload with IRStream
    bool link_relocatable(
        const dgtl::ir::IRStream& s,
        std::vector<uint8_t>& o,
        std::string_view e = "DGTL_kernel_entry") const {
        dgtl::linker::DGTLLinker l;
        return l.build_elf_relocatable_binary(s, o, e);
    }

    bool link_static_archive(
        const dgtl::ir::IRStream& s,
        std::vector<uint8_t>& o,
        std::string_view m = "module.o") const {
        dgtl::linker::DGTLLinker l;
        return l.build_static_archive_binary(s, o, m);
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::backend::linker::elf

inline constexpr uint32_t kRelocNone       = 0;

inline constexpr uint32_t kRelocPC32       = 2; // R_X86_64_PC32
inline constexpr uint32_t kRelocPLT32      = 4; // R_X86_64_PLT32
inline constexpr uint32_t kReloc32         = 10;// R_X86_64_32

[[nodiscard]] inline constexpr uint32_t to_elf_reloc_x86_64(dgtl::object::RelocationKind kind) noexcept {
    switch (kind) {
        case dgtl::object::RelocationKind::Absolute64: return 1;
        case dgtl::object::RelocationKind::PCRel32:    return 2;
        case dgtl::object::RelocationKind::Absolute32: return 10;
        case dgtl::object::RelocationKind::PLT:        return 4;
        case dgtl::object::RelocationKind::GOT:        return 3;
        default:                                       return 2;
    }
}

FILE: D:\TataG\DGTL\include\dgtl\linker\orchestrator\AOT_Orchestrator.ixx

/*
 * Copyright (c) 2026 TataG Systems. All rights reserved.
 *

 * Owner:       Divyanshu Tata

/* 
 * DGTL AOT ORCHESTRATOR
 * MODULE: dgtl.backend.orchestrator.aot
 * PURPOSE: Sovereign, industrial-grade AOT compiler orchestrator. Coordinates
 *          whole-program offline lowering, multi-module IR/MIR unification,
 *          and direct binary synthesis (.obj, .lib, .exe, .o, .a, .wasm, .macho).
 */

module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <vector>
#include <string_view>
#include <string>
#include <cstring>
#include <cstdint>
#include <iostream>
#include <chrono>
#include <algorithm>
#include <fstream>
#include <memory>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <utility>
#include <span>

#if defined(_WIN32) || defined(_WIN64)
    #include <windows.h>
    #undef CONST
    #undef IMAGE_FILE_MACHINE_AMD64
    #undef IMAGE_REL_AMD64_REL32
#else
    #include <sys/mman.h>
    #include <unistd.h>
#endif

export module dgtl.linker.aot_orchestrator;

import dgtl.ir;
import dgtl.target;
import dgtl.ir.stream;
import dgtl.linker.driver;
import dgtl.runtime.bulletins;
import dgtl.mir;
import dgtl.object.file;
import dgtl.object.writers.pe;
import dgtl.object.writers.elf;
import dgtl.object.writers.macho;
import dgtl.object.writers.wasm;
import dgtl.linker.driver_api;

export namespace dgtl::linker::aot {

struct AOTCompilationReport {
    double total_ms = 0.0;
    size_t nodes_processed = 0;
    double throughput_nodes_sec = 0.0;
    size_t text_section_bytes = 0;
    size_t rdata_section_bytes = 0;
    size_t symbol_count = 0;
    std::string target_format;
};

class DGTLAOTOrchestrator {
protected:
    dgtl::linker::driver::DGTLAOTLinker linker_;
    AOTCompilationReport last_report_{};

    [[nodiscard]] static bool write_file(std::string_view path, const std::vector<uint8_t>& bytes) {
        if (path.empty()) return false;
        std::ofstream out(std::string(path), std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
        if (!out.is_open()) return false;
        if (!bytes.empty()) {
            out.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(bytes.data()), static_cast<std::streamsize>(bytes.size()));
        }
        return out.good();
    }

public:
    DGTLAOTOrchestrator() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] const AOTCompilationReport& get_last_report() const noexcept {
        return last_report_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<std::string>& get_diagnostics() const noexcept {
        return linker_.fallback_diagnostics();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static std::unordered_map<uint32_t, std::string> merge_default_callees(
        const std::unordered_map<uint32_t, std::string>& user_callees)
    {
        std::unordered_map<uint32_t, std::string> merged = user_callees;
        static const std::pair<uint32_t, const char*> kBuiltinCallees[] = {
            {0x80000000u, "dgtl_gpu_init_pipeline"},
            {0x80000001u, "dgtl_gpu_resize"},
            {0x80000002u, "dgtl_gpu_destroy_pipeline"},
            {0x80000003u, "dgtl_gpu_begin_frame"},
            {0x80000004u, "dgtl_gpu_end_frame"},
            {0x80000005u, "dgtl_gpu_push_clip_rect"},
            {0x80000006u, "dgtl_gpu_pop_clip_rect"},
            {0x80000007u, "dgtl_gpu_render_quad_instances"},
            {0x80000008u, "dgtl_gpu_render_glyph_instances"},
            {0x80000009u, "dgtl_gpu_render_vector_paths"},
            {0x8000000Au, "dgtl_gpu_upload_texture"},
            {0x8000000Bu, "dgtl_gpu_destroy_texture"},
            {0x8000000Cu, "dgtl_platform_poll_events"},
            {0x8000000Du, "dgtl_platform_get_time_us"},
            {0x8000000Eu, "dgtl_platform_set_cursor"}
        };
        for (const auto& [slot, name] : kBuiltinCallees) {
            merged.try_emplace(slot, name);
        }
        return merged;
    }

    /**
     * @brief Sovereign MIR MachineFunction direct compilation and emission.
     */
    bool compile_and_emit(
        std::string_view output_path,
        const machine::MachineFunction& fn,
        std::string_view entry_symbol = "DGTL_kernel_entry")
    {
        if (output_path.empty()) return false;
        const auto start_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        last_report_ = AOTCompilationReport{};

        std::string path_str(output_path);
        std::string ext;
        if (const size_t dot = path_str.find_last_of('.'); dot != std::string::npos) {
            ext = path_str.substr(dot);
            std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), [](unsigned char c) {
                return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c));
            });
        }

        std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
        bool success = false;

        if (ext == ".obj") {
            last_report_.target_format = "COFF_OBJ";
            dgtl::object::writers::PEObjectWriter writer;
            success = writer.write_machine_function(fn, bytes, entry_symbol) && write_file(output_path, bytes);
        } else if (ext == ".lib") {
            last_report_.target_format = "COFF_LIB";
            dgtl::object::writers::PEObjectWriter writer;
            std::vector<uint8_t> obj_bytes;
            success = writer.write_machine_function(fn, obj_bytes, entry_symbol) &&
                      writer.build_archive(obj_bytes, bytes, "module.obj") &&
                      write_file(output_path, bytes);
        } else if (ext == ".exe") {
            last_report_.target_format = "PE_EXE";
            dgtl::object::writers::PEObjectWriter writer;
            success = writer.write_standalone_executable(fn, bytes, entry_symbol) && write_file(output_path, bytes);
        } else if (ext == ".o") {
            last_report_.target_format = "ELF_OBJ";
            dgtl::object::writers::ELFObjectWriter writer;
            success = writer.write_machine_function(fn, bytes, entry_symbol) && write_file(output_path, bytes);
        } else if (ext == ".a") {
            last_report_.target_format = "ELF_ARCHIVE";
            dgtl::object::writers::ELFObjectWriter writer;
            std::vector<uint8_t> obj_bytes;
            success = writer.write_machine_function(fn, obj_bytes, entry_symbol) &&
                      writer.build_archive(obj_bytes, bytes, "module.o") &&
                      write_file(output_path, bytes);
        } else if (ext == ".wasm") {
            last_report_.target_format = "WASM_BINARY";
            dgtl::object::writers::WASMBinaryWriter writer;
            success = writer.write_machine_function(fn, bytes) && write_file(output_path, bytes);
        } else if (ext == ".macho") {
            last_report_.target_format = "MACHO_OBJ";
            dgtl::object::writers::MachOObjectWriter writer;
            success = writer.write_machine_function(fn, bytes, entry_symbol) && write_file(output_path, bytes);
        } else {
            // Default to COFF relocatable object
            last_report_.target_format = "COFF_OBJ_DEFAULT";
            dgtl::object::writers::PEObjectWriter writer;
            success = writer.write_machine_function(fn, bytes, entry_symbol) && write_file(output_path, bytes);
        }

        const auto end_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        const std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli> elapsed = end_time - start_time;
        last_report_.total_ms = elapsed.count();
        last_report_.text_section_bytes = bytes.size();
        return success;
    }

    /**
     * @brief Universal ObjectFile direct emission.
     */
    bool compile_and_emit(
        std::string_view output_path,
        const dgtl::object::ObjectFile& obj)
    {
        if (output_path.empty()) return false;
        std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
        bool success = false;

        switch (obj.format) {
            case dgtl::object::ObjectFormat::COFF: {
                dgtl::object::writers::PEObjectWriter writer;
                success = writer.write(obj, bytes);
                break;
            }
            case dgtl::object::ObjectFormat::ELF: {
                dgtl::object::writers::ELFObjectWriter writer;
                success = writer.write(obj, bytes);
                break;
            }
            case dgtl::object::ObjectFormat::MachO: {
                dgtl::object::writers::MachOObjectWriter writer;
                success = writer.write(obj, bytes);
                break;
            }
            case dgtl::object::ObjectFormat::Wasm: {
                dgtl::object::writers::WASMBinaryWriter writer;
                success = writer.write(obj, bytes);
                break;
            }
        }
        if (success) {
            return write_file(output_path, bytes);
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * @brief Compile multiple MachineFunctions into a unified object file.
     */
    bool compile_module(
        std::string_view output_path,
        const std::vector<const machine::MachineFunction*>& functions)
    {
        if (output_path.empty() || functions.empty()) return false;
        dgtl::object::ObjectFile unified_obj;
        unified_obj.format = dgtl::object::ObjectFormat::COFF;
        dgtl::object::Section text_sec;
        text_sec.name = ".text";
        text_sec.kind = dgtl::object::SectionKind::Text;
        text_sec.alignment = 16;
        text_sec.flags = 0x60000020; // IMAGE_SCN_CNT_CODE | IMAGE_SCN_MEM_EXECUTE | IMAGE_SCN_MEM_READ

        for (const auto* fn_ptr : functions) {
            if (!fn_ptr) continue;
            const auto& fn = *fn_ptr;
            uint64_t fn_offset = text_sec.size();
            if (fn_offset % 16 != 0) {
                size_t pad = 16 - (fn_offset % 16);
                text_sec.data.insert(text_sec.data.end(), pad, 0x90);
                fn_offset = text_sec.size();
            }
            unified_obj.add_symbol(dgtl::object::Symbol::make_function(
                fn.name(), fn_offset, 0, dgtl::object::SymbolBinding::Global));
        }

        unified_obj.add_section(std::move(text_sec));
        return compile_and_emit(output_path, unified_obj);
    }

    /**
     * @brief Emit a single IRStream to a target file, dispatching automatically by file extension.
     */
    bool compile_and_emit(
        std::string_view output_path,
        const ir::IRStream& stream,
        std::string_view entry_symbol = "DGTL_kernel_entry",
        const std::unordered_map<uint32_t, std::string>& slot_to_callee = {})
    {
        if (output_path.empty() || stream.is_null() || stream.size() == 0) return false;

        const auto start_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        last_report_ = AOTCompilationReport{};
        last_report_.nodes_processed = stream.size();

        std::string path_str(output_path);
        std::string ext;
        if (const size_t dot = path_str.find_last_of('.'); dot != std::string::npos) {
            ext = path_str.substr(dot);
            std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), [](unsigned char c) {
                return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c));
            });
        }

        bool success = false;
        const std::vector<uint8_t> rdata_payload = dgtl::runtime::build_registered_strings_rdata();
        const auto effective_callees = merge_default_callees(slot_to_callee);

        if (ext == ".obj") {
            last_report_.target_format = "COFF_OBJ";
            std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
            success = linker_.build_coff_relocatable_binary_with_relocs(
                stream, bytes, entry_symbol, {}, effective_callees, rdata_payload);
            if (success) success = write_file(output_path, bytes);
        }
        else if (ext == ".lib") {
            last_report_.target_format = "COFF_LIB";
            std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
            success = linker_.build_coff_archive_binary(stream, bytes, "module.obj", entry_symbol);
            if (success) success = write_file(output_path, bytes);
        }
        else if (ext == ".exe") {
            last_report_.target_format = "PE_EXE";
            success = linker_.build_pe_executable(output_path, stream);
        }
        else if (ext == ".o") {
            last_report_.target_format = "ELF_OBJ";
            std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
            success = linker_.build_elf_relocatable_binary(stream, bytes, entry_symbol);
            if (success) success = write_file(output_path, bytes);
        }
        else if (ext == ".a") {
            last_report_.target_format = "ELF_ARCHIVE";
            std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
            success = linker_.build_static_archive_binary(stream, bytes, "module.o");
            if (success) success = write_file(output_path, bytes);
        }
        else {
            // Default to COFF relocatable object
            last_report_.target_format = "COFF_OBJ_DEFAULT";
            std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
            success = linker_.build_coff_relocatable_binary_with_relocs(
                stream, bytes, entry_symbol, {}, effective_callees, rdata_payload);
            if (success) success = write_file(output_path, bytes);
        }

        const auto end_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        const std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli> elapsed = end_time - start_time;
        last_report_.total_ms = elapsed.count();
        if (last_report_.total_ms > 1e-9) {
            last_report_.throughput_nodes_sec = (static_cast<double>(stream.size()) / (last_report_.total_ms / 1000.0));
        }

        return success;
    }

    /**
     * @brief Compile and emit a multi-function module to a dual-section COFF object.
     */
    bool compile_module_to_coff(
        std::string_view output_path,
        const std::vector<std::pair<std::string, ir::IRStream>>& module_streams,
        const std::unordered_map<uint32_t, std::string>& slot_to_callee = {})
    {
        if (output_path.empty() || module_streams.empty()) return false;

        const auto start_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        last_report_ = AOTCompilationReport{};
        last_report_.target_format = "COFF_MODULE_OBJ";

        size_t total_nodes = 0;
        for (const auto& [name, st] : module_streams) {
            if (!st.is_null()) total_nodes += st.size();
        }
        last_report_.nodes_processed = total_nodes;

        const std::vector<uint8_t> rdata_payload = dgtl::runtime::build_registered_strings_rdata();
        const auto effective_callees = merge_default_callees(slot_to_callee);
        std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;

        const bool success = linker_.build_module_coff_with_relocs(
            module_streams, bytes, effective_callees, rdata_payload);

        if (!success) return false;
        if (!write_file(output_path, bytes)) return false;

        const auto end_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        const std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli> elapsed = end_time - start_time;
        last_report_.total_ms = elapsed.count();
        if (last_report_.total_ms > 1e-9) {
            last_report_.throughput_nodes_sec = (static_cast<double>(total_nodes) / (last_report_.total_ms / 1000.0));
        }

        return true;
    }

    /**
     * @brief Compile and emit IRStream to a static library archive (.lib / .a).
     */
    bool compile_to_archive(
        std::string_view output_path,
        const ir::IRStream& stream,
        std::string_view member_name = "module.obj",
        std::string_view entry_symbol = "DGTL_kernel_entry")
    {
        if (output_path.empty() || stream.is_null() || stream.size() == 0) return false;
        std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
        bool ok = false;
        std::string path_str(output_path);
        if (path_str.size() >= 2 && path_str.substr(path_str.size() - 2) == ".a") {
            ok = linker_.build_static_archive_binary(stream, bytes, member_name.empty() ? "module.o" : member_name);
        } else {
            ok = linker_.build_coff_archive_binary(stream, bytes, member_name, entry_symbol);
        }
        if (!ok) return false;
        return write_file(output_path, bytes);
    }

    /**
     * @brief Direct-to-binary compilation for standalone executable (.exe).
     */
    bool compile_to_executable(
        std::string_view output_path,
        const ir::IRStream& stream)
    {
        if (output_path.empty() || stream.is_null() || stream.size() == 0) return false;
        return linker_.build_pe_executable(output_path, stream);
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::backend::aot

export namespace dgtl::backend::orchestrator {
    using DGTLAOTOrchestrator = dgtl::backend::aot::DGTLAOTOrchestrator;
    using AOTCompilationReport = dgtl::backend::aot::AOTCompilationReport;
}

 *              with complete embedded IAT (kernel32.dll -> ExitProcess),
 *              16-byte Win64 ABI stack alignment, ASLR support, and sub-10ms native execution.
 */

/* ============================================================================
 * PE Constants & Layout Specifications
 * ========================================================================== */

namespace pe {

inline constexpr uint16_t kDosMagic             = 0x5A4D;     // MZ
inline constexpr uint32_t kPeSignature           = 0x00004550; // PE\0\0

inline constexpr uint16_t kMachineAMD64          = 0x8664;
inline constexpr uint16_t kOptionalHeader64Magic = 0x020B;

inline constexpr uint16_t kNumberOfSections      = 4; // .text, .rdata, .idata, .reloc

inline constexpr uint16_t kCharacteristicsExecutable = 0x0002;
inline constexpr uint16_t kCharacteristicsLargeAddressAware = 0x0020;
inline constexpr uint16_t kCharacteristicsRelocsStripped = 0x0001;

inline constexpr uint16_t kSubsystemWindowsCUI = 0x0003;

inline constexpr uint16_t kDllCharHighEntropyVA = 0x0020;
inline constexpr uint16_t kDllCharDynamicBase   = 0x0040;
inline constexpr uint16_t kDllCharNXCompat      = 0x0100;
inline constexpr uint16_t kDllCharTerminalServerAware = 0x8000;

inline constexpr uint32_t kSectionMemExecute  = 0x20000000;
inline constexpr uint32_t kSectionMemRead     = 0x40000000;
inline constexpr uint32_t kSectionMemWrite    = 0x80000000;
inline constexpr uint32_t kSectionContainsCode = 0x00000020;
inline constexpr uint32_t kSectionInitializedData = 0x00000040;

inline constexpr uint32_t kDirImport    = 1;
inline constexpr uint32_t kDirBaseReloc = 5;
inline constexpr uint32_t kDirIAT      = 12;

inline constexpr uint64_t kPreferredImageBase = 0x0000000140000000ULL;
inline constexpr uint32_t kSectionAlignment   = 0x1000;
inline constexpr uint32_t kFileAlignment      = 0x200;
inline constexpr uint32_t kHeadersSize        = 0x400;
inline constexpr uint32_t kDosHeaderOffsetToNT = 0x80;
inline constexpr uint32_t kDefaultTimestamp   = 0x00000000;

inline constexpr uint32_t kStackReserve = 0x00400000; // 4MB
inline constexpr uint32_t kStackCommit  = 0x00010000;  // 64KB
inline constexpr uint32_t kHeapReserve  = 0x00100000;  // 1MB
inline constexpr uint32_t kHeapCommit   = 0x00001000;  // 4KB

} // namespace pe


/* ============================================================================
 * Low-Level Checked Arithmetic
 * ========================================================================== */

namespace detail {

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool checked_add_u32(uint32_t a, uint32_t b, uint32_t& out) noexcept {
    if (b > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max() - a) return false;
    out = a + b;
    return true;
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr uint32_t align_up(uint32_t value, uint32_t alignment) noexcept {
    if (alignment == 0) return value;
    const uint32_t mask = alignment - 1;
    if (value > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max() - mask) return 0;
    return (value + mask) & ~mask;
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_power_of_two(uint32_t value) noexcept {
    return value != 0 && (value & (value - 1)) == 0;
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool fits_int32(int64_t value) noexcept {
    return value >= std::numeric_limits<int32_t>::min() && value <= std::numeric_limits<int32_t>::max();
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool fits_uint32(uint64_t value) noexcept {
    return value <= std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max();
}

} // namespace detail


/* ============================================================================
 * Portable PE Structures
 * ========================================================================== */

struct DosHeader {
    uint16_t e_magic{};
    uint16_t e_cblp{};
    uint16_t e_cp{};
    uint16_t e_crlc{};
    uint16_t e_cparhdr{};
    uint16_t e_minalloc{};
    uint16_t e_maxalloc{};
    uint16_t e_ss{};
    uint16_t e_sp{};
    uint16_t e_csum{};
    uint16_t e_ip{};
    uint16_t e_cs{};
    uint16_t e_lfarlc{};
    uint16_t e_ovno{};
    std::array<uint16_t, 4> e_res{};
    uint16_t e_oemid{};
    uint16_t e_oeminfo{};
    std::array<uint16_t, 10> e_res2{};
    uint32_t e_lfanew{};
};

struct FileHeader {
    uint16_t Machine{};
    uint16_t NumberOfSections{};
    uint32_t TimeDateStamp{};
    uint32_t PointerToSymbolTable{};
    uint32_t NumberOfSymbols{};
    uint16_t SizeOfOptionalHeader{};
    uint16_t Characteristics{};
};

struct DataDirectory {
    uint32_t VirtualAddress{};
    uint32_t Size{};
};

struct OptionalHeader64 {
    uint16_t Magic{};
    uint8_t MajorLinkerVersion{};
    uint8_t MinorLinkerVersion{};

    uint32_t SizeOfCode{};
    uint32_t SizeOfInitializedData{};
    uint32_t SizeOfUninitializedData{};

    uint32_t AddressOfEntryPoint{};
    uint32_t BaseOfCode{};

    uint64_t ImageBase{};

    uint32_t SectionAlignment{};
    uint32_t FileAlignment{};

    uint16_t MajorOperatingSystemVersion{};
    uint16_t MinorOperatingSystemVersion{};

    uint16_t MajorImageVersion{};
    uint16_t MinorImageVersion{};

    uint16_t MajorSubsystemVersion{};
    uint16_t MinorSubsystemVersion{};

    uint32_t Win32VersionValue{};

    uint16_t Subsystem{};

    uint64_t SizeOfStackReserve{};

    uint32_t LoaderFlags{};
