enum class MachineVerifyError : std::uint8_t {
    None,
    InvalidBlock,
    InvalidInstruction,
    InvalidOperand,
    InvalidRegister,
    MissingTerminator,
    InvalidCFGEdge,
    DuplicateBlock,
    DuplicateInstruction,
    InvalidStackSlot,
    InvalidMemoryOperand,
    InvalidOperandRole,
    MissingSemanticKind,
    MissingTargetOpcode,
    InvalidTargetOpcodeState,
    InvalidPhaseTransition,
    MissingEntryBlock,
    PhaseViolation,
    CFGIntegrityViolation,
    SSADominanceViolation
};

struct MachineVerifyResult {
    MachineVerifyError error = MachineVerifyError::None;
    BlockId block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    InstrId instruction = INVALID_INSTR_ID;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool ok() const noexcept {
        return error == MachineVerifyError::None;
    }
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr dgtl::utils::BitMatrixLUT<16, 16> build_operand_role_legality_lut() noexcept {
    dgtl::utils::BitMatrixLUT<16, 16> lut{};
    for (std::size_t r = 0; r <= 7; ++r) {
        lut.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(MachineOperandKind::Register), r, true);
    }
    auto set_read_only = [&](MachineOperandKind k) {
        lut.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(k), static_cast<std::size_t>(OperandRole::Use), true);
        lut.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(k), static_cast<std::size_t>(OperandRole::Debug), true);
    };
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Immediate);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::FloatingImmediate);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Symbol);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::StackSlot);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::ConstantPoolIndex);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::RegisterMask);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Metadata);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Memory);
    return lut;
}

inline constexpr auto kOperandRoleLegalityLUT = build_operand_role_legality_lut();

inline bool MachineFunction::verify_cfg_integrity(std::string* out_error) const {
    const BlockId root = entry_block();
    if (root != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && root >= blocks().size()) {
        if (out_error) *out_error = "Entry block ID is out of range.";
        return false;
    }
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < blocks().size(); ++i) {
        const auto* bb_ptr = block(static_cast<BlockId>(i));
        if (!bb_ptr) continue;
        const auto& bb = *bb_ptr;
        if (bb.id != static_cast<BlockId>(i)) {
            if (out_error) *out_error = "Block id does not match vector index at " + std::to_string(i);
            return false;
        }
        for (BlockId succ_id : bb.successors) {
            if (succ_id >= blocks().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Successor " + std::to_string(succ_id) + " out of range in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            const auto* succ_bb = block(succ_id);
            if (!succ_bb || std::find(succ_bb->predecessors.begin(), succ_bb->predecessors.end(), bb.id) == succ_bb->predecessors.end()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "CFG asymmetry: block " + std::to_string(bb.id) + " has successor " + std::to_string(succ_id) + " but successor lacks predecessor back-edge.";
                return false;
            }
        }
        for (BlockId pred_id : bb.predecessors) {
            if (pred_id >= blocks().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Predecessor " + std::to_string(pred_id) + " out of range in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            const auto* pred_bb = block(pred_id);
            if (!pred_bb || std::find(pred_bb->successors.begin(), pred_bb->successors.end(), bb.id) == pred_bb->successors.end()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "CFG asymmetry: block " + std::to_string(bb.id) + " has predecessor " + std::to_string(pred_id) + " but predecessor lacks successor forward-edge.";
                return false;
            }
        }
        InstrId curr = bb.head_instr;
        InstrId prev = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        std::size_t counted = 0;
        while (curr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            if (curr >= instructions().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Instruction ID " + std::to_string(curr) + " out of range in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            const auto* inst = instruction(curr);
            if (!inst || inst->parent_block != bb.id) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Instruction " + std::to_string(curr) + " parent_block mismatch in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            if (inst->prev_instr != prev) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Instruction chain broken: prev_instr mismatch at instr " + std::to_string(curr);
                return false;
            }
            prev = curr;
            curr = inst->next_instr;
            counted++;
            if (counted > instructions().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Cycle detected in instruction intrusive list of block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
        }
        if (prev != bb.tail_instr) {
            if (out_error) *out_error = "Tail instruction pointer mismatch in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
            return false;
        }
        if (counted != bb.instructions.size()) {
            if (out_error) *out_error = "Intrusive chain count " + std::to_string(counted) + " does not match instructions vector size " + std::to_string(bb.instructions.size()) + " in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

inline bool MachineFunction::verify_ssa_dominance(std::string* out_error) const {
    if (!properties().is_ssa) {
        return true;
    }

    struct DefLocation {
        InstrId instr_id = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        BlockId block_id = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        std::size_t instr_index_in_block = 0;
    };

    std::unordered_map<RegId, DefLocation> vreg_defs;

    for (const auto& bb : blocks()) {
        for (std::size_t idx = 0; idx < bb.instructions.size(); ++idx) {
            const InstrId iid = bb.instructions[idx];
            const auto* inst = instruction(iid);
            if (!inst) continue;

            for (const auto& op : inst->operands) {
                if (op.is_register() && op.is_def() && op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                    const RegId r = op.reg.id;
                    if (vreg_defs.find(r) != vreg_defs.end()) {
                        if (out_error) {
                            *out_error = "SSA violation: virtual register v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                         " defined multiple times (first in instr " +
                                         std::to_string(vreg_defs[r].instr_id) + ", then in instr " +
                                         std::to_string(iid) + ").";
                        }
                        return false;
                    }
                    vreg_defs[r] = DefLocation{iid, bb.id, idx};
                }
            }
        }
    }

    const auto& dom = dominators();

    for (const auto& bb : blocks()) {
        for (std::size_t idx = 0; idx < bb.instructions.size(); ++idx) {
            const InstrId iid = bb.instructions[idx];
            const auto* inst = instruction(iid);
            if (!inst) continue;

            const bool is_phi = inst->is_phi();

            if (is_phi) {
                for (std::size_t op_idx = 1; op_idx + 1 < inst->operands.size(); op_idx += 2) {
                    const auto& block_op = inst->operands[op_idx];
                    const auto& val_op = inst->operands[op_idx + 1];
                    if (block_op.kind == MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock &&
                        val_op.is_register() && val_op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                        const RegId r = val_op.reg.id;
                        auto def_it = vreg_defs.find(r);
                        if (def_it == vreg_defs.end()) {
                            if (out_error) {
                                *out_error = "SSA use without def: virtual register v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                             " used in PHI instruction " + std::to_string(iid) + " has no definition.";
                            }
                            return false;
                        }
                        const BlockId pred_block = block_op.block;
                        const DefLocation& def_loc = def_it->second;
                        if (!dom.dominates(def_loc.block_id, pred_block)) {
                            if (out_error) {
                                *out_error = "SSA dominance violation: definition of v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                             " in block " + std::to_string(def_loc.block_id) +
                                             " does not dominate PHI incoming predecessor block " +
                                             std::to_string(pred_block) + " in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                            }
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                for (const auto& op : inst->operands) {
                    if (op.is_register() && op.is_use() && op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                        const RegId r = op.reg.id;
                        auto def_it = vreg_defs.find(r);
                        if (def_it == vreg_defs.end()) {
                            if (out_error) {
                                *out_error = "SSA use without def: virtual register v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                             " used in instruction " + std::to_string(iid) + " has no definition.";
                            }
                            return false;
                        }
                        const DefLocation& def_loc = def_it->second;
                        if (def_loc.block_id == bb.id) {
                            if (def_loc.instr_index_in_block >= idx) {
                                if (out_error) {
                                    *out_error = "SSA intra-block dominance violation: use of v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                                 " at instr " + std::to_string(iid) + " precedes its definition at instr " +
                                                 std::to_string(def_loc.instr_id);
                                }
                                return false;
                            }
                        } else {
                            if (!dom.dominates(def_loc.block_id, bb.id)) {
                                if (out_error) {
                                    *out_error = "SSA dominance violation: definition of v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                                 " in block " + std::to_string(def_loc.block_id) +
                                                 " does not dominate use block " + std::to_string(bb.id) +
                                                 " at instr " + std::to_string(iid);
                                }
                                return false;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

inline BlockBranchTopology MachineFunction::block_branch_topology(BlockId block_id) const noexcept {
    const auto* bb = block(block_id);
    if (!bb) return BlockBranchTopology::Malformed;
    if (bb->instructions.empty() || bb->tail_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::EmptyOrFallthrough;
    }
    const auto* tail = instruction(bb->tail_instr);
    if (!tail) return BlockBranchTopology::Malformed;

    if (!tail->has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Terminator)) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::EmptyOrFallthrough;
    }

    if (tail->form == MachineForm::Return || tail->has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Return)) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::Return;
    }
    if (tail->form == MachineForm::Syscall) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::SyscallOrTrap;
    }
    if (tail->form == MachineForm::Goto) {
        if (tail->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            const auto* prev = instruction(tail->prev_instr);
            if (prev && (prev->form == MachineForm::BranchCond || prev->form == MachineForm::BranchCompare)) {
                return BlockBranchTopology::ConditionalAndUnconditionalBranch;
            }
        }
        return BlockBranchTopology::UnconditionalBranch;
    }
    if (tail->form == MachineForm::BranchCond || tail->form == MachineForm::BranchCompare) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::ConditionalBranchWithFallthrough;
    }
    for (const auto& op : tail->operands) {
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex) {
            return BlockBranchTopology::IndirectBranch;
        }
    }
    return BlockBranchTopology::UnconditionalBranch;
}

class MachineVerifier {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] static MachineVerifyResult
    verify(const MachineFunction& function) noexcept {
        if (!function.verify_cfg_integrity()) {
            return {MachineVerifyError::CFGIntegrityViolation, INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
        }
        if (function.properties().is_ssa && !function.verify_ssa_dominance()) {
            return {MachineVerifyError::SSADominanceViolation, INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
        }

        const auto blocks = function.blocks();
        const auto instructions = function.instructions();

        std::vector<std::uint32_t> instruction_membership(instructions.size(), 0u);

        if (function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc)) {
            if (blocks.empty() || function.entry_block() == INVALID_BLOCK_ID ||
                function.entry_block() >= blocks.size() ||
                !blocks[function.entry_block()].entry) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::MissingEntryBlock,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
        }

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < blocks.size(); ++i) {
            if (blocks[i].id != static_cast<BlockId>(i)) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::DuplicateBlock,
                    blocks[i].id,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }

            if (blocks[i].instr_count != blocks[i].instructions.size()) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
            if (blocks[i].instructions.empty()) {
                if (blocks[i].head_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID ||
                    blocks[i].tail_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
                }
            } else if (blocks[i].head_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID ||
                       blocks[i].tail_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }

            bool seen_terminator = false;
            for (std::size_t position = 0; position < blocks[i].instructions.size(); ++position) {
                const InstrId id = blocks[i].instructions[position];
                if (id >= instructions.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        id
                    };
                }
                if (++instruction_membership[id] != 1u) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::DuplicateInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        id
                    };
                }

                const MachineInstruction& block_instr = instructions[id];

                if (block_instr.parent_block != blocks[i].id) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                if (block_instr.form == MachineForm::Unhandled) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                if (requires_semantic_kind(block_instr.form) &&
                    !block_instr.has_semantic_kind()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::MissingSemanticKind, blocks[i].id, id};
                }
                if (block_instr.has_semantic_kind() &&
                    !semantic_kind_matches_form(block_instr.form, block_instr.semantic_kind)) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                const bool emission_phase =
                    function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::FrameLowered);
                if (emission_phase && !block_instr.has_target_opcode()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::MissingTargetOpcode, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                if (seen_terminator) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        id
                    };
                }
                if (block_instr.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Terminator)) {
                    seen_terminator = true;
                    if (position + 1u != blocks[i].instructions.size()) {
                        return {
                            MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            blocks[i].id,
                            id
                        };
                    }
                }

                for (const MachineOperand& operand : block_instr.operands) {
                    switch (operand.kind) {
                    case MachineOperandKind::Register:
                        if (!operand.reg.valid() ||
                            operand.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Invalid) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidRegister, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock:
                        if (operand.block == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || operand.block >= blocks.size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::StackSlot:
                        if (operand.stack_slot == INVALID_STACK_SLOT ||
                            operand.stack_slot >= function.frame_info().slots.size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::ConstantPoolIndex:
                        if (operand.constant_pool_index >= function.constant_pool().entries().size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex:
                        if (operand.jump_table_index >= function.jump_table_info().entries().size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::Memory:
                        if (operand.memory.scale != 1 && operand.memory.scale != 2 &&
                            operand.memory.scale != 4 && operand.memory.scale != 8) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidMemoryOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        if (operand.memory.alignment == 0) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidMemoryOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        if (!operand.memory.load && !operand.memory.store &&
                            (block_instr.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Load) ||
                             block_instr.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Store))) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidMemoryOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::Invalid:
                        return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                    default:
                        break;
                    }

                    if (!kOperandRoleLegalityLUT.get(static_cast<std::size_t>(operand.kind),
                                                     static_cast<std::size_t>(operand.role))) {
                        return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperandRole, blocks[i].id, id};
                    }
                }
            }

            if (function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc) &&
                !blocks[i].instructions.empty() && !seen_terminator) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::MissingTerminator,
                        blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }

            for (BlockId successor : blocks[i].successors) {
                if (successor >= blocks.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }

            for (BlockId predecessor : blocks[i].predecessors) {
                if (predecessor >= blocks.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        for (const MachineBasicBlock& block : blocks) {
            InstrId cursor = block.head_instr;
            InstrId previous = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
            std::size_t chain_count = 0;
            while (cursor != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                if (cursor >= instructions.size() || chain_count > block.instructions.size()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            block.id, cursor};
                }
                const MachineInstruction& chained = instructions[cursor];
                if (chained.parent_block != block.id ||
                    chained.prev_instr != previous) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            block.id, cursor};
                }
                if (std::find(block.instructions.begin(),
                              block.instructions.end(), cursor) == block.instructions.end()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::DuplicateInstruction,
                            block.id, cursor};
                }
                previous = cursor;
                cursor = chained.next_instr;
                ++chain_count;
            }
            if (chain_count != block.instructions.size() ||
                previous != block.tail_instr) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        block.id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
            if (block.tail_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID &&
                instructions[block.tail_instr].next_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        block.id, block.tail_instr};
            }

            for (BlockId successor : block.successors) {
                const MachineBasicBlock& target = blocks[successor];
                if (std::find(target.predecessors.begin(),
                              target.predecessors.end(),
                              block.id) == target.predecessors.end()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        block.id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }
            for (BlockId predecessor : block.predecessors) {
                const MachineBasicBlock& source = blocks[predecessor];
                if (std::find(source.successors.begin(),
                              source.successors.end(),
                              block.id) == source.successors.end()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        block.id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < instruction_membership.size(); ++i) {
            if (instruction_membership[i] != 1u) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::DuplicateInstruction,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    static_cast<InstrId>(i)
                };
            }
        }

        for (const MachineInstruction& instr : instructions) {
            if (instr.id == INVALID_INSTR_ID ||
                instr.form == MachineForm::Unhandled) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    instr.id
                };
            }

            const bool before_target_lowering =
                static_cast<std::uint8_t>(function.phase()) <
                static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc);
            if (before_target_lowering) {
                if (is_target_opcode(instr.opcode)) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::PhaseViolation,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }
            } else {
                if (!instr.has_target_opcode()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::MissingTargetOpcode,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }
            }

            for (const MachineOperand& operand : instr.operands) {
                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                    !operand.reg.valid()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidRegister,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }

                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock &&
                    operand.block >= blocks.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidBlock,
                        operand.block,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }

                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot &&
                    operand.stack_slot >= function.frame_info().slots.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        for (const MachineStackSlot& slot : function.frame_info().slots) {
            if (slot.id == INVALID_STACK_SLOT) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }
            if (slot.alignment == 0u || slot.size_bytes == 0u) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }
        }

        if (static_cast<std::uint8_t>(function.phase()) >=
            static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::PostRegAlloc)) {
            if (function.has_virtual_registers() ||
                !function.properties().registers_allocated) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidRegister,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }
        }

        if (static_cast<std::uint8_t>(function.phase()) >=
            static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::FrameLowered)) {
            const auto& frame = function.frame_info();

            if (frame.stack_alignment == 0u) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }

            for (const auto& slot : frame.slots) {
                if (!slot.fixed_offset || slot.alignment == 0u ||
                    slot.size_bytes == 0u) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                            INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
                }
                const auto alignment = static_cast<std::int64_t>(slot.alignment);
                if (slot.offset % alignment != 0) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                            INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
                }
            }
        }

        if (function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::EmissionReady)) {
            if (!function.has_target_opcodes() || function.has_virtual_registers()) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidTargetOpcodeState,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
            if (!function.properties().frame_lowered ||
                !function.properties().abi_lowered ||
                !function.properties().registers_allocated) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::PhaseViolation,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
        }

        return {};
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::machine

 * Provides concept-constrained, zero-cost semantic views around
 * const MachineInstruction& for target lowering, instruction encoders,
 * peephole optimizers, and machine schedulers.

module;

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <concepts>
#include <span>
#include <string_view>
#include <type_traits>
#include <vector>

export module dgtl.mir.op_wrappers;

import dgtl.mir.machine_ir;

export namespace dgtl::machine::views {

inline const MachineOperand kInvalidOperand{};

/* ============================================================================
 * Concepts
 * ========================================================================== */

template <typename T>
concept MachineInstructionLike = requires(const T& t) {
    { t.operands } -> std::same_as<const std::vector<MachineOperand>&>;
    { t.form } -> std::same_as<const MachineForm&>;
    { t.opcode } -> std::same_as<const MachineOpcode&>;
    { t.traits } -> std::same_as<const MachineTrait&>;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineBinaryOpView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineBinaryOpView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineBinaryOpView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& dst() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0) ? inst_.operands[0] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& lhs() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? inst_.operands[1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& rhs() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 2) ? inst_.operands[2] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineForm form() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineOpcode opcode() const noexcept {
        return inst_.opcode;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_generic() const noexcept {
        return is_generic_opcode(inst_.opcode);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_target() const noexcept {
        return is_target_opcode(inst_.opcode);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_commutative() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_trait(MachineTrait::Commutative);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_no_wrap() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::NoWrap);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_exact() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Exact);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_fast_math() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::FastMath);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint16_t latency_cycles() const noexcept {
        return inst_.latency_cycles;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineLoadView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineLoadView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineLoadView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& dst() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0) ? inst_.operands[0] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& address_operand() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? inst_.operands[1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister base() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) return op.memory.base;
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) return op.reg;
        return {};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister index() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.index : MachineRegister{};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::int32_t scale() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.scale : 1;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::int64_t displacement() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) return op.memory.displacement;
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Immediate) return op.immediate;
        return 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t alignment() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.alignment : 1u;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] StackSlotId stack_slot() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot) ? op.stack_slot : INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_stack_load() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form == MachineForm::LoadStack || address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_volatile() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Volatile) ||
               (address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory && address_operand().memory.volatile_access);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_atomic() const noexcept {
        return inst_.is_atomic() ||
               (address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory && address_operand().memory.atomic);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineStoreView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineStoreView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineStoreView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& val() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0) ? inst_.operands[0] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& address_operand() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? inst_.operands[1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister base() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) return op.memory.base;
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) return op.reg;
        return {};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister index() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.index : MachineRegister{};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::int32_t scale() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.scale : 1;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::int64_t displacement() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) return op.memory.displacement;
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Immediate) return op.immediate;
        return 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t alignment() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.alignment : 1u;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] StackSlotId stack_slot() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot) ? op.stack_slot : INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_stack_store() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form == MachineForm::StoreStack || address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_volatile() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Volatile) ||
               (address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory && address_operand().memory.volatile_access);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_atomic() const noexcept {
        return inst_.is_atomic() ||
               (address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory && address_operand().memory.atomic);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineBranchView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineBranchView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineBranchView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] BlockId target_block() const noexcept {
        for (const auto& op : inst_.operands) {
            if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock) {
                return op.block;
            }
        }
        return INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& condition() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0 && inst_.operands[0].kind != MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock)
            ? inst_.operands[0]
            : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_conditional() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form == MachineForm::BranchCond || inst_.form == MachineForm::BranchCompare;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_fused_compare() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form == MachineForm::BranchCompare;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_likely() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::HotPath);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_unlikely() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::ColdPath);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineCallView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineCallView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineCallView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] SymbolId callee_symbol() const noexcept {
        if (!inst_.operands.empty() && inst_.operands[0].kind == MachineOperandKind::Symbol) {
            return inst_.operands[0].symbol;
        }
        return INVALID_SYMBOL;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister callee_reg() const noexcept {
        if (!inst_.operands.empty() && inst_.operands[0].kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) {
            return inst_.operands[0].reg;
        }
        return {};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t arg_count() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? (inst_.operands.size() - 1) : 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& arg(std::size_t index) const noexcept {
        return (index + 1 < inst_.operands.size()) ? inst_.operands[index + 1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_indirect() const noexcept {
        return !inst_.operands.empty() && inst_.operands[0].kind == MachineOperandKind::Register;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_syscall() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form == MachineForm::Syscall;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_variadic() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Variadic);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineFmaView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineFmaView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineFmaView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& dst() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0) ? inst_.operands[0] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& acc() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? inst_.operands[1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& mul_a() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 2) ? inst_.operands[2] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& mul_b() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 3) ? inst_.operands[3] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_fast_math() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::FastMath);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineTensorView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineTensorView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineTensorView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& output() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0) ? inst_.operands[0] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& input_a() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? inst_.operands[1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& input_b() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 2) ? inst_.operands[2] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint64_t intent_hash() const noexcept {
        return inst_.intent_hash;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineAtomicView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineAtomicView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineAtomicView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& target_mem() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0) ? inst_.operands[0] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& expected() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? inst_.operands[1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& desired() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 2) ? inst_.operands[2] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_relaxed() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Relaxed);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_acquire() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Acquire);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_release() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Release);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_seq_cst() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::SeqCst);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Factory Helpers
 * ========================================================================== */

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineBinaryOpView as_binary(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept {
    return MachineBinaryOpView(inst);
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineLoadView as_load(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept {
    return MachineLoadView(inst);
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineStoreView as_store(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept {
    return MachineStoreView(inst);
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineBranchView as_branch(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept {
    return MachineBranchView(inst);
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineCallView as_call(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept {
    return MachineCallView(inst);
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineFmaView as_fma(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept {
    return MachineFmaView(inst);
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineTensorView as_tensor(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept {
    return MachineTensorView(inst);
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineAtomicView as_atomic(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept {
    return MachineAtomicView(inst);
}

}

/*

 *
 * Project:     DGTL (Deep General-purpose Transformation Layer)
 * Owner:       Divyanshu Tata
 * Repository:  github.com/TataG-Systems/DGTL
 *
 * Component:   Universal Architecture-Independent Register Allocator
 * Standards:   C++26 / Clang 18+ / MSVC 19.40+ / GCC 14+

 * This software is the proprietary property of TataG Systems.
 * Unauthorized copying, modification, or distribution is strictly prohibited.

module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>
export module dgtl.mir.regalloc.universal;
import dgtl.mir.machine_ir;
export namespace dgtl::mir::regalloc {

using namespace dgtl::machine;

struct UniversalLiveInterval {
    MachineRegister reg{};
    std::size_t first = 0;
    std::size_t last = 0;
    std::uint32_t use_count = 0;
    bool fixed = false;
    MachineRegister fixed_target{};
};

class UniversalGraphColoringRegAlloc final : public MachineRegisterAllocator {
public:
    UniversalGraphColoringRegAlloc(
        MachineFunction& function,
        const TargetRegisterInfo& reg_info,
        const TargetInstructionInfo* instr_info = nullptr,
        const TargetFrameInfo* frame_info = nullptr,
        MachineSpiller* spiller = nullptr) noexcept
        : function_(function),
          reg_info_(reg_info),
          instr_info_(instr_info),
          frame_info_(frame_info),
          spiller_(spiller) {}

    bool run() override {
        if (!function_.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc)) {
            return false;
        }

        assignments_.clear();
        spills_.clear();
        spilled_virtuals_.clear();

        // Spilling mutates MIR. Rebuild liveness/interference and recolor until
        // all virtual registers are gone or the target spiller reports failure.
        for (std::size_t iteration = 0; iteration < kMaxAllocationIterations; ++iteration) {
            if (!function_.has_virtual_registers()) {
                function_.mark_registers_allocated();
                return function_.properties().registers_allocated;
            }

            AllocationState state;
            if (!build_interference_graph(state)) {
                return false;
            }

            if (!color_graph(state)) {
                return false;
            }

            if (state.spilled.empty()) {
                for (const auto& [v_id, p_reg] : assignments_) {
                    MachineRegister vreg{v_id, RegisterKind::Virtual, p_reg.reg_class};
                    if (!function_.replace_virtual_register(vreg, p_reg)) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }

                if (function_.has_virtual_registers()) {
                    return false;
                }

                function_.mark_registers_allocated();
                return function_.properties().registers_allocated;
            }

            if (spiller_ == nullptr || frame_info_ == nullptr) {
                return false;
            }

            for (const MachineRegister vreg : state.spilled) {
                const std::uint32_t size =
                    std::max<std::uint32_t>(1u, frame_info_->spill_size(vreg.reg_class));
                const std::uint32_t alignment =
                    std::max<std::uint32_t>(1u, frame_info_->spill_alignment(vreg.reg_class));

                const SpillRequest request{
                    vreg,
                    vreg.reg_class,
                    size,
                    alignment
                };
                const SpillResult result = spiller_->spill(function_, request);
                if (!result.spilled || result.slot == INVALID_STACK_SLOT) {
                    return false;
                }
                spills_[vreg.id] = result.slot;
                spilled_virtuals_.push_back(vreg);

                if (!spiller_->rewrite_for_spill(function_, vreg, result.slot)) {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            assignments_.clear();
        }

        return false;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<MachineRegister>
    assigned_register(MachineRegister virtual_reg) const noexcept override {
        const auto it = assignments_.find(virtual_reg.id);
        if (it != assignments_.end()) return it->second;
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<StackSlotId>
    spill_slot(MachineRegister virtual_reg) const noexcept override {
        const auto it = spills_.find(virtual_reg.id);
        if (it != spills_.end()) return it->second;
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<MachineRegister>& spilled_virtuals() const noexcept {
        return spilled_virtuals_;
    }

private:
    static constexpr std::size_t kMaxAllocationIterations = 8;

    struct AllocationState {
        std::unordered_map<std::uint32_t, MachineRegister> virtuals;
        std::unordered_map<std::uint32_t, std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t>> edges;
        std::unordered_map<std::uint32_t, std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t>> blocked_phys;
        std::unordered_map<std::uint32_t, MachineRegister> fixed;
        std::vector<MachineRegister> spilled;
    };

    static void add_edge(AllocationState& state,
                         std::uint32_t lhs,
                         std::uint32_t rhs) {
        if (lhs == rhs) return;
        state.edges[lhs].insert(rhs);
        state.edges[rhs].insert(lhs);
    }

    static void add_virtual_operand(const MachineOperand& op,
                                    std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t>& set) {
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register && op.reg.is_virtual()) {
            set.insert(op.reg.id);
        } else if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) {
            if (op.memory.base.is_virtual()) set.insert(op.memory.base.id);
            if (op.memory.index.is_virtual()) set.insert(op.memory.index.id);
        }
    }

    static void classify_operand(const MachineOperand& op,
                                 std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t>& uses,
                                 std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t>& defs) {
        auto classify_reg = [&](MachineRegister reg, OperandRole role) {
            if (!reg.is_virtual()) return;
            switch (role) {
                case OperandRole::Def:
                case OperandRole::ImplicitDef:
                case OperandRole::Dead:
                    defs.insert(reg.id);
                    break;
                case OperandRole::UseDef:
                    uses.insert(reg.id);
                    defs.insert(reg.id);
                    break;
                default:
                    uses.insert(reg.id);
                    break;
            }
        };

        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) {
            classify_reg(op.reg, op.role);
        } else if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) {
            // Address registers are reads unless a target explicitly models
            // them as a separate def operand.
            classify_reg(op.memory.base, OperandRole::Use);
            classify_reg(op.memory.index, OperandRole::Use);
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool build_interference_graph(AllocationState& state) {
        // Discover virtual registers and fixed-register constraints.
        for (const auto& block : function_.blocks()) {
            for (InstrId id : block.instructions) {
                const MachineInstruction* inst = function_.instruction(id);
                if (!inst) return false;

                for (std::size_t op_idx = 0; op_idx < inst->operands.size(); ++op_idx) {
                    const MachineOperand& op = inst->operands[op_idx];
                    if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register && op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                        state.virtuals.emplace(op.reg.id, op.reg);
                        state.edges.try_emplace(op.reg.id);
                        if (instr_info_ && inst->has_target_opcode()) {
                            const auto constraint =
                                instr_info_->register_constraint(inst->opcode, op_idx);
                            if (constraint.kind == MachineRegisterConstraintKind::FixedRegister) {
                                if (!constraint.fixed_register.valid() ||
                                    !constraint.fixed_register.is_physical()) {
                                    return false;
                                }
                                const auto it = state.fixed.find(op.reg.id);
                                if (it != state.fixed.end() &&
                                    (it->second != constraint.fixed_register) &&
                                    reg_info_.aliases(it->second, constraint.fixed_register)) {
                                    return false;
                                }
                                state.fixed[op.reg.id] = constraint.fixed_register;
                            }
                        }
                    } else if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) {
                        if (op.memory.base.is_virtual()) {
                            state.virtuals.emplace(op.memory.base.id, op.memory.base);
                            state.edges.try_emplace(op.memory.base.id);
                        }
                        if (op.memory.index.is_virtual()) {
                            state.virtuals.emplace(op.memory.index.id, op.memory.index);
                            state.edges.try_emplace(op.memory.index.id);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Build block-level use/def sets.
        std::vector<std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t>> use(function_.blocks().size());
        std::vector<std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t>> def(function_.blocks().size());
        std::vector<std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t>> live_in(function_.blocks().size());
        std::vector<std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t>> live_out(function_.blocks().size());

        for (std::size_t b = 0; b < function_.blocks().size(); ++b) {
            for (InstrId id : function_.blocks()[b].instructions) {
                const auto* inst = function_.instruction(id);
                if (!inst) return false;

                std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t> uses;
                std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t> defs;
                for (const auto& op : inst->operands) {
                    classify_operand(op, uses, defs);
                }

                for (const auto v : uses) {
                    if (!def[b].contains(v)) use[b].insert(v);
                }
                def[b].insert(defs.begin(), defs.end());
            }
        }

        // Standard backward data-flow liveness over the actual MIR CFG.
        bool changed = true;
        for (std::size_t pass = 0; pass < function_.blocks().size() + 1u && changed; ++pass) {
            changed = false;
            for (std::size_t reverse = function_.blocks().size(); reverse-- > 0;) {
                const auto& block = function_.blocks()[reverse];
                std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t> new_out;
                for (BlockId succ : block.successors) {
                    if (succ >= live_in.size()) return false;
                    new_out.insert(live_in[succ].begin(), live_in[succ].end());
                }

                std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t> new_in = use[reverse];
                for (const auto v : new_out) {
                    if (!def[reverse].contains(v)) new_in.insert(v);
                }

                if (new_out != live_out[reverse] || new_in != live_in[reverse]) {
                    live_out[reverse] = std::move(new_out);
                    live_in[reverse] = std::move(new_in);
                    changed = true;
                }
            }
        }

        // Instruction-level interference and physical-register blocking.
        for (std::size_t b = 0; b < function_.blocks().size(); ++b) {
            std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t> live = live_out[b];

            for (std::size_t reverse = function_.blocks()[b].instructions.size(); reverse-- > 0;) {
                const InstrId id = function_.blocks()[b].instructions[reverse];
                const auto* inst = function_.instruction(id);
                if (!inst) return false;

                std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t> uses;
                std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t> defs;
                std::vector<MachineRegister> physical_operands;

                for (std::size_t op_idx = 0; op_idx < inst->operands.size(); ++op_idx) {
                    const auto& op = inst->operands[op_idx];
                    classify_operand(op, uses, defs);
                    if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register && op.reg.is_physical()) {
                        physical_operands.push_back(op.reg);
                    } else if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) {
                        if (op.memory.base.is_physical()) physical_operands.push_back(op.memory.base);
                        if (op.memory.index.is_physical()) physical_operands.push_back(op.memory.index);
                    }
                }

                std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t> live_here = live;
                live_here.insert(uses.begin(), uses.end());
                live_here.insert(defs.begin(), defs.end());

                for (const auto v : defs) {
                    for (const auto other : live) add_edge(state, v, other);
                    for (const auto other : defs) add_edge(state, v, other);
                }

                for (const auto v : live_here) {
                    for (const auto phys : physical_operands) {
                        state.blocked_phys[v].insert(phys.id);
                    }
                }

                for (const auto v : defs) live.erase(v);
                live.insert(uses.begin(), uses.end());
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool candidate_matches_class(const MachineRegister& virtual_reg,
                                                const MachineRegister& physical) const noexcept {
        if (virtual_reg.reg_class == physical.reg_class) return true;
        if (virtual_reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::General &&
            (physical.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Integer ||
             physical.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Address)) {
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<MachineRegister>
    candidates_for(const MachineRegister& virtual_reg) const {
        std::vector<MachineRegister> result;
        const std::size_t count = reg_info_.physical_register_count();
        for (std::uint32_t id = 0; id < count; ++id) {
            for (RegisterClassKind cls : {
                    RegisterClassKind::General,
                    RegisterClassKind::Integer,
                    RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint,
                    RegisterClassKind::Vector,
                    RegisterClassKind::Predicate,
                    RegisterClassKind::Mask,
                    RegisterClassKind::Address,
                    RegisterClassKind::Special,
                    RegisterClassKind::Custom}) {
                MachineRegister candidate{id, RegisterKind::Physical, cls};
                if (!reg_info_.is_allocatable(candidate) ||
                    reg_info_.is_reserved(candidate) ||
                    !candidate_matches_class(virtual_reg, candidate)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (reg_info_.register_class(candidate) != cls) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (std::find(result.begin(), result.end(), candidate) == result.end()) {
                    result.push_back(candidate);
                }
            }
        }
        return result;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool color_graph(AllocationState& state) {
        std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t> uncolored;
        for (const auto& [id, _] : state.virtuals) uncolored.insert(id);

        while (!uncolored.empty()) {
            std::uint32_t chosen = INVALID_REG;
            std::size_t best_saturation = 0;
            std::size_t best_degree = 0;

            for (const auto id : uncolored) {
                std::unordered_set<std::uint32_t> saturation;
                for (const auto neighbor : state.edges[id]) {
                    const auto assigned = assignments_.find(neighbor);
                    if (assigned != assignments_.end()) {
                        saturation.insert(assigned->second.id);
                    }
                }

                const std::size_t degree = state.edges[id].size();
                if (chosen == INVALID_REG ||
                    saturation.size() > best_saturation ||
                    (saturation.size() == best_saturation && degree > best_degree)) {
                    chosen = id;
                    best_saturation = saturation.size();
                    best_degree = degree;
                }
            }

            const MachineRegister vreg = state.virtuals.at(chosen);
            std::vector<MachineRegister> candidates = candidates_for(vreg);

            const auto fixed_it = state.fixed.find(chosen);
            if (fixed_it != state.fixed.end()) {
                candidates.erase(
                    std::remove_if(candidates.begin(), candidates.end(),
                                   [&](const MachineRegister& c) {
                                       return !(c == fixed_it->second);
                                   }),
                    candidates.end());
            }

            std::optional<MachineRegister> selected;
            for (const MachineRegister candidate : candidates) {
                bool conflict = false;
                const auto blocked = state.blocked_phys.find(chosen);
                if (blocked != state.blocked_phys.end() &&
                    blocked->second.contains(candidate.id)) {
                    conflict = true;
                }

                if (!conflict) {
                    for (const auto neighbor : state.edges[chosen]) {
                        const auto assigned = assignments_.find(neighbor);
                        if (assigned != assignments_.end() &&
                            (assigned->second == candidate ||
                             reg_info_.aliases(assigned->second, candidate))) {
                            conflict = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (!conflict) {
                    selected = candidate;
                    break;
                }
            }

            uncolored.erase(chosen);

            if (selected.has_value()) {
                assignments_[chosen] = *selected;
            } else {
                state.spilled.push_back(vreg);
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    MachineFunction& function_;
    const TargetRegisterInfo& reg_info_;
    const TargetInstructionInfo* instr_info_;
    const TargetFrameInfo* frame_info_;
    MachineSpiller* spiller_;

    std::unordered_map<std::uint32_t, MachineRegister> assignments_;
    std::unordered_map<std::uint32_t, StackSlotId> spills_;
    std::vector<MachineRegister> spilled_virtuals_;
};

} // namespace dgtl::isa::regalloc::regalloc

export namespace dgtl::mir::regalloc {
    using UniversalGraphColoringRegAlloc = dgtl::isa::regalloc::regalloc::UniversalGraphColoringRegAlloc;
}
