#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <string_view>

export module dgtl.mir.runtime_contract;

// Pure Machine IR imports only - zero upstream ir::OpCode dependencies
import dgtl.mir.machine_ir;
import dgtl.target;

/**
 * @brief Backend Runtime Contract
 *
 * Defines the contract between compiled Machine IR instructions and the host runtime.
 * Implements direct mapping of target-independent GenericMachineOpcodes (GM_*) and
 * MachineSemanticKind operations to pre-compiled runtime helper symbols.
 * Zero dependency on high-level ir::OpCode.
 */
export namespace dgtl::mir::runtime_contract {

inline constexpr std::string_view kRuntimeContractVersion = "3";

namespace detail {

using namespace dgtl::machine;

inline constexpr size_t kMaxGenericOpcodeIndex = 256;

// Cacheline-aligned table mapping GenericMachineOpcode directly to runtime symbols
alignas(64) inline constexpr auto kRuntimeSymbolTable = []() constexpr {
    std::array<const char*, kMaxGenericOpcodeIndex> table{};
    table.fill(nullptr);

    // String Manipulation
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_STRING_LENGTH)]      = "__dgtl_rt_string_length";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_STRING_COMPARE)]     = "__dgtl_rt_string_compare";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_STRING_CONCAT)]      = "__dgtl_rt_string_concat";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_STRING_SUBSTRING)]   = "__dgtl_rt_string_substring";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_STRING_COPY)]        = "__dgtl_rt_string_copy";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_STRING_EQUALS)]      = "__dgtl_rt_string_equals";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_STRING_FIND)]        = "__dgtl_rt_string_find";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_STRING_HASH)]        = "__dgtl_rt_string_hash";

    // Fences & Synchronization
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_FENCE)]              = "__dgtl_rt_memory_fence";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_ACQUIRE_FENCE)]      = "__dgtl_rt_acquire_fence";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_RELEASE_FENCE)]      = "__dgtl_rt_release_fence";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_ATOMIC_FENCE)]       = "__dgtl_rt_atomic_fence";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_BARRIER_SYNC)]       = "__dgtl_rt_barrier_sync";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_SYNC_BARRIER)]       = "__dgtl_rt_sync_barrier";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_LOCK_ACQUIRE)]       = "__dgtl_rt_lock_acquire";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_LOCK_RELEASE)]       = "__dgtl_rt_lock_release";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_SPIN_ACQUIRE)]       = "__dgtl_rt_spin_acquire";

    // Atomics
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_ATOMIC_LOAD)]        = "__dgtl_rt_atomic_load";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_ATOMIC_STORE)]       = "__dgtl_rt_atomic_store";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_ATOMIC_SNAPSHOT)]    = "__dgtl_rt_atomic_snapshot";

    // Async, Fibers, Coroutines, Threads
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_ASYNC_AWAIT)]        = "__dgtl_rt_async_await";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_ASYNC_LAUNCH)]       = "__dgtl_rt_async_launch";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_ASYNC_SPAWN)]        = "__dgtl_rt_async_spawn";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_FIBER_YIELD)]        = "__dgtl_rt_fiber_yield";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_FIBER_YIELD_LOOP)]   = "__dgtl_rt_fiber_yield_loop";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_SUSPEND_CTX)]        = "__dgtl_rt_suspend_ctx";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_RESUME_CTX)]         = "__dgtl_rt_resume_ctx";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_THREAD_SPAWN)]       = "__dgtl_rt_thread_spawn";

    // Dynamic Allocations, Data Structures, Graphs, Trees
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_DSA_ALLOCATE)]       = "__dgtl_rt_dsa_allocate";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_DSA_DEALLOCATE)]     = "__dgtl_rt_dsa_deallocate";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_RING_PUSH_FREE)]     = "__dgtl_rt_ring_push_free";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_DEOPT_TRIGGER)]      = "__dgtl_rt_deopt_trigger";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TREE_BUILD)]         = "__dgtl_rt_tree_build";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TREE_TRAVERSE)]      = "__dgtl_rt_tree_traverse";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TRIE_BUILD)]         = "__dgtl_rt_trie_build";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TRIE_INSERT)]        = "__dgtl_rt_trie_insert";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TRIE_LOOKUP)]        = "__dgtl_rt_trie_lookup";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_GRAPH_BUILD)]        = "__dgtl_rt_graph_build";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_GRAPH_TRAVERSE)]     = "__dgtl_rt_graph_traverse";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TUPLE_PACK)]         = "__dgtl_rt_tuple_pack";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TUPLE_UNPACK)]       = "__dgtl_rt_tuple_unpack";

    // Math & Float Conversions / Intrinsics
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_FFMA32)]             = "__dgtl_rt_fma_f32";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_FFMA64)]             = "__dgtl_rt_fma_f64";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_FAST_INV_SQRT)]      = "__dgtl_rt_fast_inv_sqrt";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_VEC_GATHER)]         = "__dgtl_rt_gather_f32_512";

    // Memory Helpers & Cache Intrinsics
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_LEA)]                = "__dgtl_rt_lea";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_GEP)]                = "__dgtl_rt_gep";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_PTR_DIFF)]           = "__dgtl_rt_ptr_diff";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_PTR_TO_INT)]         = "__dgtl_rt_ptr_to_int";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_INT_TO_PTR)]         = "__dgtl_rt_int_to_ptr";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_PREFETCH)]           = "__dgtl_rt_prefetch";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_PREFETCH_T0)]        = "__dgtl_rt_prefetch_t0";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_CLFLUSH)]            = "__dgtl_rt_clflush";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_READ_TSC)]           = "__dgtl_rt_read_tsc";

    // Tensor Operations & Accelerators
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_OP)]          = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_gemm";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_BROADCAST)]   = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_broadcast";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_CONCAT)]      = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_concat";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_CONV)]        = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_conv";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_GEMM)]        = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_gemm";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_GATHER)]      = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_gather";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_POOL)]        = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_pool";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_REDUCE)]      = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_reduce";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_RESHAPE)]     = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_reshape";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_SCATTER_D)]   = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_scatter_d";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_SLICE)]       = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_slice";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_SOFTMAX)]     = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_softmax";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_TENSOR_TRANSPOSE)]   = "__dgtl_rt_tensor_transpose";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_MATMUL_BF16)]        = "__dgtl_rt_matmul_bf16";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_MATMUL_FP8)]         = "__dgtl_rt_matmul_fp8";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_SDPA_F16)]           = "__dgtl_rt_sdpa_f16";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_SOFTMAX_FAST)]       = "__dgtl_rt_softmax_fast";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_LAYER_NORM)]         = "__dgtl_rt_layer_norm";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_ROPE_EMBED)]         = "__dgtl_rt_rope_embed";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_MAT4_MUL)]           = "__dgtl_rt_mat4_mul";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_QUAT_SLERP)]         = "__dgtl_rt_quat_slerp";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_VEC3_CROSS)]         = "__dgtl_rt_vec3_cross";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_VEC4_DOT)]           = "__dgtl_rt_vec4_dot";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_RAY_AABB_ISECT)]     = "__dgtl_rt_ray_aabb_isect";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_GPU_LAUNCH)]         = "__dgtl_rt_gpu_launch";
    table[static_cast<size_t>(GM_WASM_INVOKE)]        = "__dgtl_rt_wasm_invoke";

    return table;
}();

} // namespace detail

/**
 * @brief Resolves a GenericMachineOpcode to its runtime ABI symbol name.
 * Operates strictly on MachineOpcode with zero upstream IR dependencies.
 */
[[nodiscard]] inline const char* runtime_symbol_for_opcode(dgtl::machine::MachineOpcode opcode) noexcept {
    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(opcode);
    if (index < detail::kRuntimeSymbolTable.size()) [[likely]] {
        return detail::kRuntimeSymbolTable[index];
    }
    return nullptr;
}

/**
 * @brief Checks if a GenericMachineOpcode maps to an external runtime library symbol.
 */
[[nodiscard]] inline bool has_runtime_symbol(dgtl::machine::MachineOpcode opcode) noexcept {
    return runtime_symbol_for_opcode(opcode) != nullptr;
}

/**
 * @brief Resolves target-independent machine semantic operations to runtime ABI symbol names.
 */
[[nodiscard]] inline const char* runtime_symbol_for_semantic_kind(dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind kind) noexcept {
    using K = dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind;
    switch (kind) {
        // Trigonometry
        case K::FloatSin:      return "__dgtl_rt_sin";
        case K::FloatCos:      return "__dgtl_rt_cos";
        case K::FloatTan:      return "__dgtl_rt_tan";
        case K::FloatAsin:     return "__dgtl_rt_asin";
        case K::FloatAcos:     return "__dgtl_rt_acos";
        case K::FloatAtan:     return "__dgtl_rt_atan";
        case K::FloatAtan2:    return "__dgtl_rt_atan2";
        case K::FloatSinh:     return "__dgtl_rt_sinh";
        case K::FloatCosh:     return "__dgtl_rt_cosh";
        case K::FloatTanh:     return "__dgtl_rt_tanh";

        // Logarithmic, Exponential & Algebra
        case K::FloatLog:      return "__dgtl_rt_log";
        case K::FloatLog2:     return "__dgtl_rt_log2";
        case K::FloatLog10:    return "__dgtl_rt_log10";
        case K::FloatLog1p:    return "__dgtl_rt_log1p";
        case K::FloatExp:      return "__dgtl_rt_exp";
        case K::FloatExp2:     return "__dgtl_rt_exp2";
        case K::FloatExp10:    return "__dgtl_rt_exp10";
        case K::FloatExpm1:    return "__dgtl_rt_expm1";
        case K::FloatPow:      return "__dgtl_rt_pow";
        case K::FloatCbrt:     return "__dgtl_rt_cbrt";
        case K::FloatHypot:    return "__dgtl_rt_hypot";

        // String Operations
        case K::StringLength:    return "__dgtl_rt_string_length";
        case K::StringCompare:   return "__dgtl_rt_string_compare";
        case K::StringConcat:    return "__dgtl_rt_string_concat";
        case K::StringSubstring: return "__dgtl_rt_string_substring";
        case K::StringCopy:      return "__dgtl_rt_string_copy";
        case K::StringEquals:    return "__dgtl_rt_string_equals";
        case K::StringFind:      return "__dgtl_rt_string_find";
        case K::StringHash:      return "__dgtl_rt_string_hash";

        // Bit Manipulation Fallbacks
        case K::IntegerPopcount: return "__dgtl_rt_popcount";
        case K::IntegerClz:      return "__dgtl_rt_clz";
        case K::IntegerCtz:      return "__dgtl_rt_ctz";

        default:
            return nullptr;
    }
}

[[nodiscard]] inline bool has_runtime_symbol_for_semantic(dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind kind) noexcept {
    return runtime_symbol_for_semantic_kind(kind) != nullptr;
}

/**
 * @brief Returns whether a semantic operation can be expanded inline into hardware
 * instructions on the specified target machine, avoiding runtime library calls.
 */
[[nodiscard]] inline bool can_expand_inline(
    const core::TargetMachine& target,
    dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind kind) noexcept {
    using K = dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind;

    switch (kind) {
        case K::FloatFma:
            return target.supports_fma() ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;

        case K::VectorAdd:
        case K::VectorMul:
            return target.description().supports_vector_width(512) ||
                   target.description().supports_vector_width(256) ||
                   target.has_feature(core::TargetFeature::NEON) ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;

        case K::VectorGather:
        case K::VectorStore:
            return target.description().supports_vector_width(512) ||
                   (target.has_feature(core::TargetFeature::AVX2) && target.supports_vector_memory()) ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;

        case K::FloatRsqrt:
            return target.family() == core::TargetFamily::X64 ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ARM64 ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;

        case K::IntegerPopcount:
            return target.has_feature(core::TargetFeature::BMI2) ||
                   target.has_feature(core::TargetFeature::NEON) ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;

        case K::IntegerClz:
        case K::IntegerCtz:
            return target.has_feature(core::TargetFeature::BMI2) ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ARM64 ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;

        case K::TensorOp:
            return target.has_feature(core::TargetFeature::Tensor) ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;

        case K::HardwareFence:
        case K::AtomicOp:
        case K::ConcurrentSync:
            return target.has_feature(core::TargetFeature::Atomics) ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                   target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;

        default:
            return false;
    }
}

} // namespace dgtl::mir::runtime_contract

FILE: D:\TataG\DGTL\include\dgtl\mir\transforms\Phi_Lowering.ixx
==================================================
/*
 * Copyright (c) 2026 TataG Systems. All rights reserved.
 *
 * Project:     DGTL (Deep General-purpose Transformation Layer)
 * Owner:       Divyanshu Tata
 * Repository:  github.com/TataG-Systems/DGTL
 *
 * Component:   Machine IR SSA Deconstruction & Critical-Edge-Aware Phi Lowering
 * Standards:   C++26 / Clang 18+ / MSVC 19.40+ / GCC 14+
 *
 * This software is the proprietary property of TataG Systems.
 * Unauthorized copying, modification, or distribution is strictly prohibited.
 */
module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdint>
#include <deque>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

export module dgtl.mir.phi_lowering;

import dgtl.mir.machine_ir;

export namespace dgtl::mir::lowering {

using dgtl::machine::BlockId;
using dgtl::machine::InstrId;
using dgtl::machine::RegId;
using dgtl::machine::MachineBasicBlock;
using dgtl::machine::MachineFunction;
using dgtl::machine::MachineInstrFlag;
using dgtl::machine::MachineInstruction;
using dgtl::machine::MachineOperand;
using dgtl::machine::MachineOperandKind;
using dgtl::machine::MachineRegister;
using dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind;
using dgtl::machine::INVALID_INSTR_ID;
using dgtl::machine::MachineForm;
using dgtl::machine::GM_COPY;
using dgtl::machine::GM_GOTO;
using dgtl::machine::GM_NOOP;

struct PendingCopy {
    MachineOperand dest{};
    MachineOperand src{};
    InstrId origin_phi = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
};

class MachinePhiLowering {
public:
    /**
     * @brief Deconstructs all SSA Phi nodes across the MachineFunction into parallel copies.
     * Implements critical-edge splitting and topological cycle breaking with scratch temporaries.
     */
    static bool run(MachineFunction& function) {
        if (!function.properties().is_ssa) {
            return true; // Already lowered from SSA
        }

        bool modified = false;

        // Step 1: Global dead-PHI pruning
        std::unordered_set<InstrId> all_phis;
        std::unordered_map<RegId, uint32_t> non_phi_use_counts;

        const auto blocks = function.blocks();
        for (std::size_t b_idx = 0; b_idx < blocks.size(); ++b_idx) {
            const MachineBasicBlock* b = function.block(static_cast<BlockId>(b_idx));
            if (!b) continue;

            for (const InstrId inst_id : b->instructions) {
                const auto* inst = function.instruction(inst_id);
                if (!inst) continue;

                if (inst->is_phi()) {
                    all_phis.insert(inst_id);
                } else {
                    for (const auto& op : inst->operands) {
                        if (op.is_register() && op.is_use() && op.reg.valid()) {
                            non_phi_use_counts[op.reg.id]++;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        std::unordered_set<InstrId> dead_phis;
        bool changed_dead = true;
        while (changed_dead) {
            changed_dead = false;
            for (const InstrId phi_id : all_phis) {
                if (dead_phis.count(phi_id)) continue;
                const auto* phi_inst = function.instruction(phi_id);
                if (!phi_inst || phi_inst->operands.empty()) {
                    dead_phis.insert(phi_id);
                    changed_dead = true;
                    continue;
                }
                const auto& dest = phi_inst->operands[0];
                if (dest.is_register() && dest.reg.valid()) {
                    uint32_t total_uses = non_phi_use_counts[dest.reg.id];
                    for (const InstrId other_id : all_phis) {
                        if (other_id == phi_id || dead_phis.count(other_id)) continue;
                        const auto* o_inst = function.instruction(other_id);
                        if (!o_inst) continue;
                        for (std::size_t op_i = 1; op_i < o_inst->operands.size(); ++op_i) {
                            const auto& op = o_inst->operands[op_i];
                            if (op.is_register() && op.reg == dest.reg) {
                                total_uses++;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (total_uses == 0) {
                        dead_phis.insert(phi_id);
                        changed_dead = true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Step 2: Lower live PHIs block by block
        for (std::size_t b_idx = 0; b_idx < blocks.size(); ++b_idx) {
            const BlockId block_id = static_cast<BlockId>(b_idx);
            MachineBasicBlock* bb = function.block(block_id);
            if (!bb) continue;

            std::vector<InstrId> phi_ids;
            for (const InstrId instr_id : bb->instructions) {
                if (all_phis.count(instr_id) && !dead_phis.count(instr_id)) {
                    phi_ids.push_back(instr_id);
                }
            }

            if (phi_ids.empty()) {
                for (const InstrId instr_id : bb->instructions) {
                    if (dead_phis.count(instr_id)) {
                        auto* inst = function.instruction(instr_id);
                        if (inst) {
                            inst->form = MachineForm::NoOp;
                            inst->semantic_kind = MachineSemanticKind::Other;
                            inst->opcode = GM_NOOP;
                            inst->operands.clear();
                        }
                        modified = true;
                    }
                }
                bb->instructions.erase(
                    std::remove_if(bb->instructions.begin(), bb->instructions.end(),
                                   [&](InstrId id) { return dead_phis.count(id) > 0; }),
                    bb->instructions.end());
                bb->instr_count = bb->instructions.size();
                continue;
            }

            std::unordered_map<BlockId, std::vector<PendingCopy>> edge_copies;

            for (const InstrId phi_id : phi_ids) {
                const auto* phi_inst = function.instruction(phi_id);
                if (!phi_inst || phi_inst->operands.empty()) continue;

                const MachineOperand dest = phi_inst->operands[0];

                for (std::size_t op_idx = 1; op_idx + 1 < phi_inst->operands.size(); op_idx += 2) {
                    const auto& block_op = phi_inst->operands[op_idx];
                    const auto& val_op = phi_inst->operands[op_idx + 1];

                    if (block_op.kind == MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock) {
                        const BlockId pred_id = block_op.block;
                        edge_copies[pred_id].push_back(PendingCopy{dest, val_op, phi_id});
                    }
                }
            }

            for (auto& [pred_id, copies] : edge_copies) {
                MachineBasicBlock* pred_bb = function.block(pred_id);
                if (!pred_bb) continue;

                const bool is_critical = (pred_bb->successors.size() > 1) && (bb->predecessors.size() > 1);

                if (is_critical) {
                    MachineBasicBlock& split_block = function.create_block(false);
                    const BlockId split_id = split_block.id;

                    for (auto& succ : pred_bb->successors) {
                        if (succ == block_id) {
                            succ = split_id;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    split_block.add_predecessor(pred_id);
                    split_block.add_successor(block_id);

                    for (auto& pred : bb->predecessors) {
                        if (pred == pred_id) {
                            pred = split_id;
                            break;
                        }
                    }

                    MachineInstruction& goto_inst = function.create_instruction();
                    goto_inst.form = MachineForm::Goto;
                    goto_inst.semantic_kind = MachineSemanticKind::Goto;
                    goto_inst.opcode = GM_GOTO;
                    goto_inst.add_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Branch | MachineInstrFlag::Terminator);
                    goto_inst.add_operand(MachineOperand::block_ref(block_id));
                    function.insert_instruction_before(split_id, INVALID_INSTR_ID, goto_inst.id);

                    emit_parallel_copies(function, split_id, copies, false);
                } else if (pred_bb->successors.size() == 1) {
                    emit_parallel_copies(function, pred_id, copies, false);
                } else {
                    emit_parallel_copies(function, block_id, copies, true);
                }

                modified = true;
            }

            for (const InstrId phi_id : bb->instructions) {
                if (all_phis.count(phi_id)) {
                    auto* inst = function.instruction(phi_id);
                    if (inst) {
                        inst->form = MachineForm::NoOp;
                        inst->semantic_kind = MachineSemanticKind::Other;
                        inst->opcode = GM_NOOP;
                        inst->operands.clear();
                    }
                }
            }

            bb->instructions.erase(
                std::remove_if(bb->instructions.begin(), bb->instructions.end(),
                               [&](InstrId id) { return all_phis.count(id) > 0; }),
                bb->instructions.end());
            bb->instr_count = bb->instructions.size();
        }

        function.properties().is_ssa = false;
        return modified;
    }

private:
    static void emit_parallel_copies(
        MachineFunction& function,
        BlockId target_block,
        std::vector<PendingCopy>& copies,
        bool at_entry = false) {

        if (copies.empty()) return;

        MachineBasicBlock* bb = function.block(target_block);
        if (!bb) return;

        InstrId cursor = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        if (at_entry) {
            for (const InstrId id : bb->instructions) {
                const auto* inst = function.instruction(id);
                if (!inst) continue;
                if (!inst->is_phi()) {
                    cursor = id;
                    break;
                }
            }
        } else {
            for (const InstrId id : bb->instructions) {
                const auto* inst = function.instruction(id);
                if (inst && inst->has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Terminator)) {
                    cursor = id;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        std::vector<PendingCopy> pending;
        pending.reserve(copies.size());
        for (const auto& copy : copies) {
            if (copy.dest.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                copy.src.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                copy.dest.reg == copy.src.reg) {
                continue;
            }
            pending.push_back(copy);
        }

        auto emit_move = [&](const MachineOperand& dst, const MachineOperand& src) {
            MachineInstruction& move_inst = function.create_instruction();
            move_inst.form = MachineForm::Move;
            move_inst.semantic_kind = MachineSemanticKind::Move;
            move_inst.opcode = GM_COPY;
            move_inst.add_operand(dst);
            move_inst.add_operand(src);
            function.insert_instruction_before(target_block, cursor, move_inst.id);
        };

        while (!pending.empty()) {
            bool progress = false;

            for (auto it = pending.begin(); it != pending.end(); ++it) {
                bool dest_read_by_others = false;
                if (it->dest.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                    const auto dest_reg = it->dest.reg;
                    for (auto other_it = pending.begin(); other_it != pending.end(); ++other_it) {
                        if (other_it != it &&
                            other_it->src.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                            other_it->src.reg == dest_reg) {
                            dest_read_by_others = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (!dest_read_by_others) {
                    emit_move(it->dest, it->src);
                    pending.erase(it);
                    progress = true;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (!progress) {
                auto& cycle_copy = pending.front();
                if (cycle_copy.dest.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                    const auto victim_reg = cycle_copy.dest.reg;
                    const MachineRegister scratch = function.create_virtual_register(victim_reg.reg_class);

                    emit_move(MachineOperand::def(scratch), MachineOperand::use(victim_reg));

                    for (auto& p : pending) {
                        if (p.src.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register && p.src.reg == victim_reg) {
                            p.src = MachineOperand::use(scratch);
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    emit_move(cycle_copy.dest, cycle_copy.src);
                    pending.erase(pending.begin());
                }
            }
        }
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::mir::lowering

/*

 *
 * Project:     DGTL (Deep General-purpose Transformation Layer)
 * Owner:       Divyanshu Tata
 * Repository:  github.com/TataG-Systems/DGTL
 *
 * Component:   Pure Machine IR Stack Spiller & Lexical Scope Mapper
 * Standards:   C++26 / Clang 18+ / MSVC 19.40+ / GCC 14+
 *
 * This software is the proprietary property of TataG Systems.
 * Unauthorized copying, modification, or distribution is strictly prohibited.
 */

module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <limits>
#include <span>
#include <vector>
#include <string_view>
#include <optional>
#include <unordered_map>

export module dgtl.mir.stack_spiller;

import dgtl.mir.machine_ir;
import dgtl.arena;

export namespace dgtl::mir::regalloc {

using dgtl::machine::RegisterClassKind;
using dgtl::machine::StackSlotId;
using dgtl::machine::INVALID_STACK_SLOT;

/**
 * @struct StackSlot
 * @brief Represents a single variable's allocation within a stack frame.
 * Aligned to 16 bytes for SIMD-safe spills.
 */
struct alignas(16) StackSlot {
    int32_t offset = 0;
    uint32_t size = 0;
    RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::General;
    uint32_t version = 0;
    bool is_spill = false;
};

/**
 * @struct SpillLifetime
 * @brief Live interval segment and requirements for a spilled virtual register.
 */
struct SpillLifetime {
    uint32_t virtual_id = 0;
    uint32_t start_point = 0;
    uint32_t end_point = 0;
    uint32_t size_bytes = 8;
    uint32_t alignment = 8;
    RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::General;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool overlaps(const SpillLifetime& other) const noexcept {
        return !(end_point <= other.start_point || other.end_point <= start_point);
    }
};

/**
 * @class StackPiller
 * @brief Manages the lexical lifecycle of symbols and their physical stack offsets.
 */
class alignas(64) StackPiller {
public:
    using ScopeLayer = std::unordered_map<uint64_t, StackSlot>;
    static constexpr uint32_t STACK_ALIGNMENT = 16;

    explicit StackPiller(dgtl::core::DGTL_Arena& arena) 
        : m_arena(arena), m_current_frame_size(0) {
        m_scope_stack.reserve(16);
        m_scope_frame_checkpoints.reserve(16);
        
        m_scope_stack.emplace_back();
        m_scope_frame_checkpoints.push_back(0);
    }

    void push_scope() {
        m_scope_stack.emplace_back();
        m_scope_frame_checkpoints.push_back(m_current_frame_size);
    }

    void pop_scope() {
        if (m_scope_stack.size() > 1) [[likely]] {
            m_scope_stack.pop_back();
            m_current_frame_size = m_scope_frame_checkpoints.back();
            m_scope_frame_checkpoints.pop_back();
        }
    }

    int32_t allocate(std::string_view name, uint32_t bytes, RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::General) {
        if (bytes == 0) [[unlikely]] {
            return 0;
        }

        const uint32_t aligned_frame = align_up(m_current_frame_size, STACK_ALIGNMENT);
        if (aligned_frame < m_current_frame_size) [[unlikely]] {
            return 0;
        }

        if (bytes > (std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max)() - aligned_frame) [[unlikely]] {
            return 0;
        }

        const uint32_t next_frame_size = aligned_frame + bytes;
        if (next_frame_size > static_cast<uint32_t>((std::numeric_limits<int32_t>::max)())) [[unlikely]] {
            return 0;
        }

        int32_t offset = -static_cast<int32_t>(next_frame_size);
        m_current_frame_size = next_frame_size;

        if (name.empty()) {
            return offset;
        }

        uint64_t name_hash = hash_name(name);
        uint32_t version = 1;
        for (auto it = m_scope_stack.rbegin(); it != m_scope_stack.rend(); ++it) {
            if (auto found = it->find(name_hash); found != it->end()) {
                version = found->second.version + 1;
                break;
            }
        }

        StackSlot slot{
            .offset = offset,
            .size = bytes,
            .reg_class = reg_class,
            .version = version,
            .is_spill = false
        };

        m_scope_stack.back()[name_hash] = slot;
        return offset;
    }

    int32_t allocate_spill(uint32_t virtual_id, uint32_t bytes = 8, RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::General) {
        const int32_t offset = allocate("", bytes, reg_class);
        const uint64_t spill_token = 0xFFFF000000000000ULL | static_cast<uint64_t>(virtual_id);
        
        StackSlot slot{
            .offset = offset,
            .size = bytes,
            .reg_class = reg_class,
            .version = 1,
            .is_spill = true
        };
        m_scope_stack.back()[spill_token] = slot;
        return offset;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::unordered_map<uint32_t, int32_t> allocate_colored_spills(
        std::span<const SpillLifetime> lifetimes);

    void bind_explicit_slot(std::string_view name, int32_t offset, uint32_t bytes, RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::General) {
        if (name.empty()) [[unlikely]] return;

        uint64_t name_hash = hash_name(name);
        StackSlot slot{
            .offset = offset,
            .size = bytes,
            .reg_class = reg_class,
            .version = 1,
            .is_spill = false
        };
        m_scope_stack.back()[name_hash] = slot;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<StackSlot> resolve(std::string_view name) const noexcept {
        uint64_t name_hash = hash_name(name);

        for (auto it = m_scope_stack.rbegin(); it != m_scope_stack.rend(); ++it) {
            if (auto found = it->find(name_hash); found != it->end()) {
                return found->second;
            }
        }
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] uint32_t current_frame_size() const noexcept {
        return m_current_frame_size;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] size_t nesting_depth() const noexcept {
        return m_scope_stack.size();
    }

    void reset_frame() {
        m_current_frame_size = 0;
        m_scope_stack.clear();
        m_scope_frame_checkpoints.clear();
        
        m_scope_stack.reserve(16);
        m_scope_frame_checkpoints.reserve(16);
        
        m_scope_stack.emplace_back();
        m_scope_frame_checkpoints.push_back(0);
    }

private:
    dgtl::core::DGTL_Arena& m_arena;
    std::vector<ScopeLayer> m_scope_stack;
    std::vector<uint32_t> m_scope_frame_checkpoints;
    uint32_t m_current_frame_size;

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr uint64_t hash_name(std::string_view name) noexcept {
        uint64_t hash = 0xcbf29ce484222325ULL;
        for (char c : name) {
            hash ^= static_cast<uint64_t>(static_cast<unsigned char>(c));
            hash *= 0x100000001b3ULL;
        }
        return hash;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr uint32_t align_up(uint32_t value, uint32_t alignment) noexcept {
        if (alignment == 0) [[unlikely]] {
            return value;
        }
        const uint32_t mask = alignment - 1U;
        return (value + mask) & ~mask;
    }
};

struct ColoredSlot {
    int32_t offset = 0;
    uint32_t size_bytes = 0;
    uint32_t alignment = 8;
    std::vector<SpillLifetime> occupants;
};

class StackSlotColorer {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] static std::unordered_map<uint32_t, int32_t> color_spill_slots(
        StackPiller& spiller,
        std::span<const SpillLifetime> lifetimes) {

        std::unordered_map<uint32_t, int32_t> assignments;
        if (lifetimes.empty()) return assignments;

        std::vector<SpillLifetime> sorted(lifetimes.begin(), lifetimes.end());
        std::sort(sorted.begin(), sorted.end(), [](const SpillLifetime& a, const SpillLifetime& b) {
            if (a.start_point != b.start_point) return a.start_point < b.start_point;
            return a.size_bytes > b.size_bytes;
        });

        std::vector<ColoredSlot> slots;

        for (const auto& item : sorted) {
            bool placed = false;

            for (auto& slot : slots) {
                if (slot.size_bytes < item.size_bytes || slot.alignment < item.alignment) {
                    continue;
                }

                bool conflict = false;
                for (const auto& occ : slot.occupants) {
                    if (occ.overlaps(item)) {
                        conflict = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (!conflict) {
                    slot.occupants.push_back(item);
                    assignments[item.virtual_id] = slot.offset;
                    placed = true;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (!placed) {
                const int32_t new_offset = spiller.allocate_spill(item.virtual_id, item.size_bytes, item.reg_class);
                ColoredSlot new_slot{};
                new_slot.offset = new_offset;
                new_slot.size_bytes = item.size_bytes;
                new_slot.alignment = item.alignment;
                new_slot.occupants.push_back(item);
                slots.push_back(new_slot);
                assignments[item.virtual_id] = new_offset;
            }
        }

        return assignments;
    }
};

inline std::unordered_map<uint32_t, int32_t> StackPiller::allocate_colored_spills(
    std::span<const SpillLifetime> lifetimes) {
    return StackSlotColorer::color_spill_slots(*this, lifetimes);
}

using StackSpiller = StackPiller;

} // namespace dgtl::mir::regalloc

/*
 * Copyright (c) 2026 TataG Systems. All rights reserved.
 *
 * Project:     DGTL (Deep General-purpose Transformation Layer)
 * Owner:       Divyanshu Tata
 * Repository:  github.com/TataG-Systems/DGTL
 *
 * Component:   Pure Machine IR Live Interval & Register Liveness Manager
 * Standards:   C++26 / Clang 18+ / MSVC 19.40+ / GCC 14+
 *
 * This software is the proprietary property of TataG Systems.
 * Unauthorized copying, modification, or distribution is strictly prohibited.
 */

module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <algorithm>

export module dgtl.mir.strike_live_manager;

import dgtl.mir.machine_ir;

export namespace dgtl::mir::regalloc {

using dgtl::machine::InstrId;
using dgtl::machine::RegId;
using dgtl::machine::BlockId;
using dgtl::machine::MachineFunction;
using dgtl::machine::MachineBasicBlock;
using dgtl::machine::MachineInstruction;
using dgtl::machine::MachineOperand;
using dgtl::machine::MachineRegister;
using dgtl::machine::INVALID_INSTR_ID;
using dgtl::machine::INVALID_REG;
using dgtl::machine::INVALID_BLOCK_ID;

struct LiveRangeSegment {
    InstrId start = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    InstrId end = INVALID_INSTR_ID;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool contains(InstrId point) const noexcept {
        return point >= start && point <= end;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool overlaps(const LiveRangeSegment& other) const noexcept {
        return !(end < other.start || other.end < start);
    }
};

struct LiveInterval {
    InstrId start_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    InstrId end_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    bool active = false;
    double spill_weight = 0.0;
    uint32_t max_loop_depth = 0;
    std::vector<LiveRangeSegment> segments;

    void add_segment(InstrId start, InstrId end) {
        if (start > end) return;
        for (auto& seg : segments) {
            if (seg.overlaps(LiveRangeSegment{start, end}) ||
                seg.end + 1 == start || end + 1 == seg.start) {
                seg.start = std::min(seg.start, start);
                seg.end = std::max(seg.end, end);
                start_instr = std::min(start_instr, seg.start);
                end_instr = std::max(end_instr, seg.end);
                return;
            }
        }
        segments.push_back(LiveRangeSegment{start, end});
        if (!active) {
            start_instr = start;
            end_instr = end;
            active = true;
        } else {
            start_instr = std::min(start_instr, start);
            end_instr = std::max(end_instr, end);
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_live_at(InstrId point) const noexcept {
        if (!active || point < start_instr || point > end_instr) {
            return false;
        }
        if (segments.empty()) {
            return true;
        }
        for (const auto& seg : segments) {
            if (seg.contains(point)) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
};

class StrikeLiveManager {
    std::vector<LiveInterval> intervals_;

public:
    /**
     * @brief Computes register live intervals directly across the MachineFunction CFG.
     * Operates strictly on MachineOperand def/use roles with zero knowledge of frontend IR.
     */
    void compute(const MachineFunction& function) {
        intervals_.clear();
        const std::size_t vreg_count = function.virtual_register_count();
        intervals_.resize(vreg_count);

        const auto& loop_info = function.loop_info();

        // 1. Traverse all blocks and instructions to establish defs and uses
        for (const auto& bb : function.blocks()) {
            const uint32_t depth = loop_info.get_loop_depth(bb.id);
            const double depth_multiplier = std::pow(10.0, static_cast<double>(std::min(depth, 5u)));

            for (const InstrId inst_id : bb.instructions) {
                const auto* inst = function.instruction(inst_id);
                if (!inst) continue;

                for (const MachineOperand& op : inst->operands) {
                    if (!op.is_register() || !op.reg.valid()) continue;

                    const RegId r = op.reg.id;
                    if (r >= intervals_.size()) {
                        intervals_.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(r) + 1);
                    }

                    auto& interval = intervals_[r];
                    interval.max_loop_depth = std::max(interval.max_loop_depth, depth);
                    interval.spill_weight += depth_multiplier;

                    if (op.is_def()) {
                        interval.add_segment(inst_id, inst_id);
                    } else if (op.is_use()) {
                        const InstrId start = (interval.start_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) ? interval.start_instr : inst_id;
                        interval.add_segment(start, inst_id);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Loop-carried live range extension (Natural loops)
        for (const auto& loop : loop_info.loops()) {
            if (loop.blocks.empty()) continue;

            InstrId loop_start = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
            InstrId loop_end = INVALID_INSTR_ID;

            for (const BlockId b : loop.blocks) {
                const auto* bb = function.block(b);
                if (!bb || bb->instructions.empty()) continue;

                const InstrId first = bb->instructions.front();
                const InstrId last = bb->instructions.back();

                loop_start = (loop_start == INVALID_INSTR_ID) ? first : std::min(loop_start, first);
                loop_end = (loop_end == INVALID_INSTR_ID) ? last : std::max(loop_end, last);
            }

            if (loop_start != INVALID_INSTR_ID && loop_end != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                for (auto& interval : intervals_) {
                    if (interval.active && interval.start_instr <= loop_end && interval.end_instr >= loop_start) {
                        interval.add_segment(loop_start, loop_end);
                        interval.max_loop_depth = std::max(interval.max_loop_depth, loop.depth);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. Normalize spill weights by live interval lifetime span
        for (auto& interval : intervals_) {
            if (interval.active) {
                const uint32_t span = (interval.end_instr >= interval.start_instr)
                    ? (interval.end_instr - interval.start_instr + 1)
                    : 1;
                interval.spill_weight /= static_cast<double>(span);
            }
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] inline bool is_dead(RegId reg_id, InstrId current_instr) const noexcept {
        if (reg_id == INVALID_REG || static_cast<std::size_t>(reg_id) >= intervals_.size()) {
            return true;
        }
        const auto& interval = intervals_[reg_id];
        if (!interval.active) {
            return true;
        }
        return current_instr >= interval.end_instr || !interval.is_live_at(current_instr);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] inline const LiveInterval* get_interval(RegId reg_id) const noexcept {
        if (reg_id == INVALID_REG || static_cast<std::size_t>(reg_id) >= intervals_.size()) {
            return nullptr;
        }
        const auto& interval = intervals_[reg_id];
        return interval.active ? &interval : nullptr;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] inline double spill_weight(RegId reg_id) const noexcept {
        const auto* interval = get_interval(reg_id);
        return interval ? interval->spill_weight : 0.0;
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::mir::regalloc

/*
 * Copyright (c) 2026 TataG Systems. All rights reserved.
 *
 * Project:     DGTL (Deep General-purpose Transformation Layer)
 * Owner:       Divyanshu Tata
 * Repository:  github.com/TataG-Systems/DGTL
 *
 * Component:   Two-Address Instruction Normalization Pass
 * Standards:   C++26 / Clang 18+ / MSVC 19.40+ / GCC 14+
 *
 * This software is the proprietary property of TataG Systems.
 * Unauthorized copying, modification, or distribution is strictly prohibited.
 *DO not use any ir opcodes instead mir opcodes valid 
 */
module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

export module dgtl.mir.two_address;

import dgtl.mir.machine_ir;
import dgtl.target;

export namespace dgtl::mir::lowering {

using dgtl::machine::BlockId;
using dgtl::machine::InstrId;
using dgtl::machine::MachineFunction;
using dgtl::machine::MachineInstrFlag;
using dgtl::machine::MachineInstruction;
using dgtl::machine::MachineOperand;
using dgtl::machine::MachineOperandKind;
using dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind;
using dgtl::machine::MachineForm;
using dgtl::machine::GM_COPY;

class TwoAddressInstructionPass {
public:
    /**
     * @brief Normalizes 3-address instructions into 2-address form for destructive targets (e.g. x86-64).
     * Eliminates redundant copies via commutation where possible, and inserts tied-operand moves when necessary.
     */
    static bool run(MachineFunction& function, const core::TargetMachine& target) {
        const auto arch = target.get_arch();

        // 3-address architectures (AArch64, ARM32, RISC-V, CUDA, ROCm, FPGA, WASM)
        // do not require destructive 2-address tied-operand normalization.
        if (arch == core::ArchType::AARCH64_SVE ||
            arch == core::ArchType::ARM32_NEON ||
            arch == core::ArchType::RISCV64_RVV ||
            arch == core::ArchType::CUDA_PTX ||
            arch == core::ArchType::ROCM_AMDGCN ||
            arch == core::ArchType::FPGA_VERILOG ||
            arch == core::ArchType::WASM_SIMD) {
            return false;
        }

        bool modified = false;

        for (const auto& block : function.blocks()) {
            const BlockId block_id = block.id;

            // Snapshot instruction list because we may insert move instructions
            const std::vector<InstrId> inst_ids = block.instructions;

            for (const InstrId inst_id : inst_ids) {
                auto* inst = function.instruction(inst_id);
                if (!inst) continue;

                // Only normalize binary instructions with at least [Def, Use1, Use2]
                if (!is_two_address_candidate(*inst)) {
                    continue;
                }

                if (inst->operands.size() < 3) continue;

                auto& def_op = inst->operands[0];
                auto& use1_op = inst->operands[1];
                auto& use2_op = inst->operands[2];

                if (def_op.kind != MachineOperandKind::Register ||
                    use1_op.kind != MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                    continue;
                }

                // If already 2-address (dest == use1), no transformation is needed
                if (def_op.reg == use1_op.reg) {
                    continue;
                }

                const bool commutable = is_commutable(*inst);

                // Commutation optimization 1: If dest == use2 and operation is commutable, swap operands!
                if (commutable && use2_op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                    def_op.reg == use2_op.reg) {
                    std::swap(inst->operands[1], inst->operands[2]);
                    modified = true;
                    continue;
                }

                // Commutation optimization 2: If use1 is immediate/non-reg but use2 is register,
                // swap so destination can be tied to the register operand.
                if (commutable && use1_op.kind != MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                    use2_op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                    std::swap(inst->operands[1], inst->operands[2]);
                    modified = true;
                    if (def_op.reg == inst->operands[1].reg) {
                        continue;
                    }
                }

                // Insert a copy: dest = Move use1 before the instruction
                MachineInstruction& copy_inst = function.create_instruction();
                copy_inst.form = MachineForm::Move;
                copy_inst.semantic_kind = MachineSemanticKind::Move;
                copy_inst.opcode = GM_COPY;
                copy_inst.add_operand(MachineOperand::def(def_op.reg));
                copy_inst.add_operand(MachineOperand::use(inst->operands[1].reg));

                function.insert_instruction_before(block_id, inst_id, copy_inst.id);

                // Tie use1 to dest register
                inst->operands[1] = MachineOperand::use(def_op.reg);
                modified = true;
            }
        }

        return modified;
    }

private:
    [[nodiscard]] static bool is_two_address_candidate(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept {
        switch (inst.form) {
            case MachineForm::IntegerBinary32:
            case MachineForm::IntegerBinary64:
            case MachineForm::FloatBinary32:
            case MachineForm::FloatBinary64:
                return true;
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static bool is_commutable(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept {
        if (inst.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Commutable)) {
            return true;
        }

        switch (inst.semantic_kind) {
            case MachineSemanticKind::IntegerAdd:
            case MachineSemanticKind::IntegerMul:
            case MachineSemanticKind::IntegerAnd:
            case MachineSemanticKind::IntegerOr:
            case MachineSemanticKind::IntegerXor:
            case MachineSemanticKind::FloatAdd:
            case MachineSemanticKind::FloatMul:
                return true;
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::mir::lowering

/*

 *
 * Project:     DGTL (Deep General-purpose Transformation Layer)
 * Owner:       Divyanshu Tata
 * Repository:  github.com/TataG-Systems/DGTL
 *
 * Component:   Universal Object File Container (ELF/COFF/Mach-O/WASM)

 * This software is the proprietary property of TataG Systems.
 * Unauthorized copying, modification, or distribution is strictly prohibited.

module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

export module dgtl.object.file;

import dgtl.object.section;
import dgtl.object.symbol;
import dgtl.object.relocation;
import dgtl.target;
import dgtl.mir.machine_ir;

export namespace dgtl::object {

enum class ObjectFormat : std::uint8_t {
    ELF,
    COFF,
    MachO,
    WASM
};

class ObjectFile {
    ObjectFormat format_ = ObjectFormat::COFF;
    std::vector<Section> sections_;
    std::vector<Symbol> symbols_;
    std::vector<Relocation> relocations_;

public:
    ObjectFile() = default;
    explicit ObjectFile(ObjectFormat fmt) : format_(fmt) {}

    [[nodiscard]] ObjectFormat format() const noexcept { return format_; }
    void set_format(ObjectFormat fmt) noexcept { format_ = fmt; }

    Section& add_section(std::string name, std::uint64_t align = 16u, std::uint32_t flags = SectionFlags::Code | SectionFlags::Read | SectionFlags::Executable, SectionKind kind = SectionKind::Text) {
        sections_.emplace_back(std::move(name), align, flags, kind);
        return sections_.back();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] Section* find_section(std::string_view name) noexcept {
        for (auto& sec : sections_) {
            if (sec.name == name) return &sec;
        }
        return nullptr;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const Section* find_section(std::string_view name) const noexcept {
        for (const auto& sec : sections_) {
            if (sec.name == name) return &sec;
        }
        return nullptr;
    }

    Section& get_or_create_section(std::string_view name, SectionKind kind = SectionKind::Text) {
        if (auto* sec = find_section(name)) return *sec;
        std::uint32_t flags = SectionFlags::Read;
        if (kind == SectionKind::Text) flags |= SectionFlags::Code | SectionFlags::Executable;
        else if (kind == SectionKind::Data || kind == SectionKind::BSS) flags |= SectionFlags::Write;
        return add_section(std::string(name), 16u, flags, kind);
    }

    Section& get_text_section() {
        return get_or_create_section(format_ == ObjectFormat::MachO ? "__text" : ".text", SectionKind::Text);
    }

    Section& get_data_section() {
        return get_or_create_section(format_ == ObjectFormat::MachO ? "__data" : ".data", SectionKind::Data);
    }

    Section& get_rdata_section() {
        return get_or_create_section(format_ == ObjectFormat::COFF ? ".rdata" : (format_ == ObjectFormat::MachO ? "__const" : ".rodata"), SectionKind::RData);
    }

    Symbol& add_symbol(std::string name, std::uint64_t value, std::uint64_t size, std::uint32_t binding = 0, std::uint32_t type = 0) {
        symbols_.emplace_back(std::move(name), value, size, binding, type, false);
        return symbols_.back();
    }

    Symbol& add_function_symbol(std::string name, std::uint64_t offset, std::uint64_t size, bool is_global = true) {
        symbols_.push_back(Symbol::make_function(std::move(name), offset, size, is_global));
        return symbols_.back();
    }

    Symbol& add_external_symbol(std::string name,
                                 std::uint32_t binding = 1,
                                 std::uint32_t type = 0) {
        const SymbolType symbol_type =
            type == 2 ? SymbolType::Function :
            type == 1 ? SymbolType::Object :
            type == 3 ? SymbolType::Section :
            type == 4 ? SymbolType::File :
            type == 5 ? SymbolType::Common :
            type == 6 ? SymbolType::TLS :
                        SymbolType::NoType;
        symbols_.push_back(Symbol::make_external(
            std::move(name), symbol_type, binding));
        return symbols_.back();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] Symbol* find_symbol(std::string_view name) noexcept {
        for (auto& sym : symbols_) {
            if (sym.name == name) return &sym;
        }
        return nullptr;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const Symbol* find_symbol(std::string_view name) const noexcept {
        for (const auto& sym : symbols_) {
            if (sym.name == name) return &sym;
        }
        return nullptr;
    }

    void add_relocation(std::uint64_t offset, std::string sym_name, std::int64_t addend = 0, std::uint32_t type = 0, RelocationKind kind = RelocationKind::PCRel32) {
        relocations_.emplace_back(offset, std::move(sym_name), addend, type, kind);
    }

    void add_relocation(Relocation rel) {
        relocations_.push_back(std::move(rel));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<Section>& sections() const noexcept { return sections_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<Section>& sections() noexcept { return sections_; }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<Symbol>& symbols() const noexcept { return symbols_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<Symbol>& symbols() noexcept { return symbols_; }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<Relocation>& relocations() const noexcept { return relocations_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<Relocation>& relocations() noexcept { return relocations_; }

    /**
     * @brief Construct an ObjectFile directly from a finalized MIR MachineFunction.
     */
    static ObjectFile from_machine_function(
        const machine::MachineFunction& function,
        ObjectFormat fmt = ObjectFormat::COFF) {

        ObjectFile obj(fmt);
        auto& text = obj.get_text_section();
        const std::uint64_t func_offset = text.size();

        // Register function symbol
        obj.add_function_symbol("DGTL_kernel_entry", func_offset, 0u, true);

        // Scan machine instructions for symbol references and record relocations
        for (const auto& inst : function.instructions()) {
            for (const auto& op : inst.operands) {
                if (op.kind == machine::MachineOperandKind::Symbol) {
                    const std::string sym_name = "sym_" + std::to_string(op.symbol);
                    if (!obj.find_symbol(sym_name)) {
                        obj.add_external_symbol(sym_name);
                    }
                    // The common MIR does not expose encoder byte offsets.  Do not
                    // manufacture a relocation location from section.size().
                    // Target emitters/object writers must attach the relocation at
                    // the exact encoded operand offset.
                    (void)sym_name;
                }
            }
        }

        return obj;
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::object

module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>

export module dgtl.object.relocation;

export namespace dgtl::object {

enum class RelocationKind : std::uint16_t {
    None = 0,
    Absolute32,
    Absolute64,
    Relative64,
    PCRel32,
    PCRel64,
    Branch26,
    Call26,
    GOT32,
    GOT64,
    PLT32,
    PLT64,
    TLS,
    High20,
    Low12,
    SecRel32
};

struct RelocationPolicy {
    bool pc_relative = false;
    std::uint8_t width = 4u;
    bool signed_value = true;
};

struct Relocation {
    std::uint64_t offset = 0u;
    std::string symbol_name;
    std::uint32_t symbol_index = 0u;
    std::int64_t addend = 0;
    std::uint32_t type = 0u;
    RelocationKind kind = RelocationKind::PCRel32;
    std::uint32_t section_index = 0u;

    Relocation() = default;
    Relocation(std::uint64_t o, std::string sym, std::int64_t add = 0, std::uint32_t t = 0, RelocationKind k = RelocationKind::PCRel32)
        : offset(o), symbol_name(std::move(sym)), addend(add), type(t), kind(k) {}

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_pc_relative() const noexcept {
        return kind == RelocationKind::PCRel32 || kind == RelocationKind::PCRel64 ||
               kind == RelocationKind::Branch26 || kind == RelocationKind::Call26 ||
               kind == RelocationKind::PLT32;
    }
};

class RelocationApplier {
public:
    static bool apply_pc32(std::uint8_t* target, std::uint64_t pc, std::uint64_t dest, std::int64_t addend = 0) noexcept {
        if (!target) return false;
        const std::int64_t delta = static_cast<std::int64_t>(dest) + addend - static_cast<std::int64_t>(pc);
        if (delta < INT32_MIN || delta > INT32_MAX) return false;
        const auto val = static_cast<std::int32_t>(delta);
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
            target[i] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((val >> (i * 8)) & 0xFF);
        }
        return true;
    }

    static bool apply_abs64(std::uint8_t* target, std::uint64_t value) noexcept {
        if (!target) return false;
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            target[i] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((value >> (i * 8)) & 0xFF);
        }
        return true;
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::object

FILE: D:\TataG\DGTL\include\dgtl\object\Object_Section.ixx

/*
 * Copyright (c) 2026 TataG Systems. All rights reserved.
 *
 * Project:     DGTL (Deep General-purpose Transformation Layer)
 * Owner:       Divyanshu Tata
 * Repository:  github.com/TataG-Systems/DGTL
 *
 * Component:   Object Section Definition & Operations
 * Standards:   C++26 / Clang 18+ / MSVC 19.40+ / GCC 14+
 *
 * This software is the proprietary property of TataG Systems.
 * Unauthorized copying, modification, or distribution is strictly prohibited.
 */

module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

export module dgtl.object.section;

export namespace dgtl::object {

enum class SectionKind : std::uint8_t {
    Text,
    Data,
    BSS,
    RData,
    Relocations,
    SymbolTable,
    StringTable,
    Debug,
    TLS,
    Custom
};

enum SectionFlags : std::uint32_t {
    None        = 0x00000000u,
    Code        = 0x00000001u,
    Read        = 0x00000002u,
    Write       = 0x00000004u,
    Executable  = 0x00000008u,
    ZeroFill    = 0x00000010u,
    Alloc       = 0x00000020u,
    ThreadLocal = 0x00000040u,
    Mergeable   = 0x00000080u,
    Strings     = 0x00000100u,
    Discardable = 0x00000200u,
    Shared      = 0x00000400u
};

struct Section {
    std::string name;
    SectionKind kind = SectionKind::Text;
    std::uint32_t flags = SectionFlags::Code | SectionFlags::Read | SectionFlags::Executable;
    std::uint64_t alignment = 16u;
    std::uint64_t address = 0u;
    std::uint64_t bss_size = 0u;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes;

    Section() = default;
    Section(std::string n, std::uint64_t a = 16u,
            std::uint32_t f = SectionFlags::Code | SectionFlags::Read | SectionFlags::Executable,
            SectionKind k = SectionKind::Text)
        : name(std::move(n)), kind(k), flags(f), alignment(a) {}

    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t size() const noexcept {
        return (kind == SectionKind::BSS) ? static_cast<std::size_t>(bss_size) : bytes.size();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept {
        return (kind == SectionKind::BSS) ? (bss_size == 0) : bytes.empty();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_code() const noexcept {
        return (flags & SectionFlags::Code) != 0 || kind == SectionKind::Text;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_writable() const noexcept {
        return (flags & SectionFlags::Write) != 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_executable() const noexcept {
        return (flags & SectionFlags::Executable) != 0 || is_code();
    }

    void emit_u8(std::uint8_t b) {
        bytes.push_back(b);
    }

    void emit_u16(std::uint16_t val) {
        bytes.push_back(static_cast<std::uint8_t>(val & 0xFFu));
        bytes.push_back(static_cast<std::uint8_t>((val >> 8u) & 0xFFu));
    }

    void emit_u32(std::uint32_t val) {
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
            bytes.push_back(static_cast<std::uint8_t>((val >> (i * 8u)) & 0xFFu));
        }
    }

    void emit_u64(std::uint64_t val) {
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            bytes.push_back(static_cast<std::uint8_t>((val >> (i * 8u)) & 0xFFu));
        }
    }

    void append_bytes(const std::uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) {
        if (data && len > 0) {
            bytes.insert(bytes.end(), data, data + len);
        }
    }

    void align_to(std::uint64_t align) {
        if (align <= 1) return;
        const std::uint64_t rem = bytes.size() % align;
        if (rem != 0) {
            const std::uint64_t pad = align - rem;
            bytes.insert(bytes.end(), pad, 0u);
        }
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::object

/*
 * Copyright (c) 2026 TataG Systems. All rights reserved.
 *
 * Project:     DGTL (Deep General-purpose Transformation Layer)
 * Owner:       Divyanshu Tata
 * Repository:  github.com/TataG-Systems/DGTL
 *
 * Component:   Object Symbol Definition & Models
 * Standards:   C++26 / Clang 18+ / MSVC 19.40+ / GCC 14+
 *
 * This software is the proprietary property of TataG Systems.
 * Unauthorized copying, modification, or distribution is strictly prohibited.
 */

module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>

export module dgtl.object.symbol;

export namespace dgtl::object {

enum class SymbolBinding : std::uint8_t {
    Local,
    Global,
    Weak,
    Hidden
};

enum class SymbolType : std::uint8_t {
    NoType,
    Object,
    Function,
    Section,
    File,
    Common,
    TLS
};

enum class SymbolVisibility : std::uint8_t {
    Default,
    Internal,
    Hidden,
    Protected
};

struct Symbol {
    std::string name;
    std::uint64_t value = 0u;
    std::uint64_t size = 0u;
    std::uint32_t section_index = 0u;
    std::uint32_t binding_raw = 0u;
    std::uint32_t type_raw = 0u;
    SymbolBinding binding = SymbolBinding::Global;
    SymbolType type = SymbolType::Function;
    SymbolVisibility visibility = SymbolVisibility::Default;
    bool external = false;

    Symbol() = default;
    Symbol(std::string n, std::uint64_t v, std::uint64_t s, std::uint32_t b = 0, std::uint32_t t = 0, bool ext = false)
        : name(std::move(n)), value(v), size(s), binding_raw(b), type_raw(t),
          binding(decode_binding(b)),
          type(decode_type(t)),
          external(ext) {}

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr SymbolBinding decode_binding(std::uint32_t raw) noexcept {
        switch (raw) {
            case 0: return SymbolBinding::Local;
            case 2: return SymbolBinding::Weak;
            case 3: return SymbolBinding::Hidden;
            default: return SymbolBinding::Global;
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr SymbolType decode_type(std::uint32_t raw) noexcept {
        switch (raw) {
            case 1: return SymbolType::Object;
            case 2: return SymbolType::Function;
            case 3: return SymbolType::Section;
            case 4: return SymbolType::File;
            case 5: return SymbolType::Common;
            case 6: return SymbolType::TLS;
            default: return SymbolType::NoType;
        }
    }

    static Symbol make_function(std::string n, std::uint64_t offset, std::uint64_t sz, bool is_global = true) {
        Symbol s;
        s.name = std::move(n);
        s.value = offset;
        s.size = sz;
        s.binding = is_global ? SymbolBinding::Global : SymbolBinding::Local;
        s.binding_raw = is_global ? 1u : 0u;
        s.type = SymbolType::Function;
        s.type_raw = 2u;
        s.external = false;
        return s;
    }

    static Symbol make_external(std::string n,
                                SymbolType t = SymbolType::NoType,
                                std::uint32_t binding_raw_value = 1u) {
        Symbol s;
        s.name = std::move(n);
        s.value = 0u;
        s.size = 0u;
        s.binding_raw = binding_raw_value;
        s.type = t;
        s.type_raw = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(t == SymbolType::Function ? 2u :
                                                 t == SymbolType::Object ? 1u : 0u);
        s.binding = decode_binding(binding_raw_value);
        s.external = true;
        return s;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_function() const noexcept { return type == SymbolType::Function; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool is_object() const noexcept { return type == SymbolType::Object; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool is_global() const noexcept { return binding == SymbolBinding::Global; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool is_local() const noexcept { return binding == SymbolBinding::Local; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool is_weak() const noexcept { return binding == SymbolBinding::Weak; }
};

} // namespace dgtl::object

#  define DGTL_MKTYPE(n)   ((dgtl_type_t){(uint32_t)(n)})

    DGTL_API void dgtl_func_builder_destroy(dgtl_func_builder_t* fb) DGTL_NOEXCEPT;
